=== API/Auth/AccessAttributes.cs ===
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Filters;
using RondiTrack.Telemetry;   // NEW 6.2

namespace RondiTrack.Auth;

// Base: runs BEFORE the action (before model binding), reads {stokvelId} from the route
public abstract class StokvelAccessFilterBase(string policy) : Attribute, IAsyncAuthorizationFilter
{
    public async Task OnAuthorizationAsync(AuthorizationFilterContext context)
    {
        // fail closed: no usable stokvel id means no access
        if (!Guid.TryParse(context.RouteData.Values["stokvelId"]?.ToString(), out var stokvelId))
            throw new DomainNotFoundException("Stokvel was not found.");

        var access = context.HttpContext.RequestServices.GetRequiredService<IStokvelAccess>();
        await access.EnsureAsync(context.HttpContext.User, stokvelId, policy);
    }
}

// Use on an endpoint: any member of the stokvel
public sealed class RequireStokvelMemberAttribute() : StokvelAccessFilterBase(Policies.StokvelMember);

// Use on an endpoint: Treasurer or Admin of the stokvel
public sealed class RequireStokvelOfficerAttribute() : StokvelAccessFilterBase(Policies.StokvelOfficer);

// Use on /api/users/{id} endpoints: your own account only
public sealed class RequireUserSelfAttribute : Attribute, IAsyncAuthorizationFilter
{
    public async Task OnAuthorizationAsync(AuthorizationFilterContext context)
    {
        var authorization = context.HttpContext.RequestServices.GetRequiredService<IAuthorizationService>();

        // an unreadable id becomes Guid.Empty, which matches nobody (fails closed)
        Guid.TryParse(context.RouteData.Values["id"]?.ToString(), out var userId);

        var result = await authorization.AuthorizeAsync(context.HttpContext.User, new UserResource(userId), Policies.UserSelf);
        if (!result.Succeeded)
        {
            RondiMetrics.AuthorizationRefused(RefusalResults.Forbidden);   // NEW 6.2: count the refusal
            throw new ForbiddenException("You can only access your own account.");   // 403 even if the id does not exist
        }
    }
}
=== API/Auth/AuthConstants.cs ===
namespace RondiTrack.Auth;

public static class AuthConstants
{
    public const string PlatformAdminRole = "PlatformAdmin";   // the ONLY role in the token (platform level)
    public const string RefreshCookieName = "rondi_refresh";   // name of the refresh cookie
    public const string RefreshCookiePath = "/api/auth";       // cookie is only sent to the auth endpoints
}

// Policy names live here, so controllers never contain role strings
public static class Policies
{
    public const string StokvelMember = "StokvelMember";           // any member of the stokvel
    public const string StokvelOfficer = "StokvelOfficer";         // Treasurer or Admin of the stokvel
    public const string RecordContribution = "RecordContribution"; // own contribution, or officer for others
    public const string UserSelf = "UserSelf";                     // your own account (or platform admin)
    public const string PlatformAdmin = "PlatformAdmin";           // platform admin claim
}
=== API/Auth/AuthExceptions.cs ===
namespace RondiTrack.Auth;

// Same message for unknown email AND wrong password, so nobody can learn which emails have accounts
public sealed class InvalidCredentialsException() : Exception("Invalid email or password.");

// Used for a missing, unknown, used, revoked or expired refresh token
public sealed class InvalidRefreshTokenException() : Exception("The refresh token is invalid or has expired.");

// Becomes a 403 problem response
public sealed class ForbiddenException(string message) : Exception(message);
=== API/Auth/AuthService.cs ===
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
using RondiTrack.Dtos;

namespace RondiTrack.Auth;

// What the controller needs after login or refresh
public sealed record AuthResult(
    string AccessToken,
    DateTime AccessTokenExpiresAtUtc,
    string RefreshToken,              // goes ONLY into the cookie
    DateTime RefreshTokenExpiresAtUtc,
    Guid RefreshTokenId);

public interface IAuthService
{
    Task<User> RegisterAsync(RegisterRequest request);
    Task<AuthResult> LoginAsync(LoginRequest request);
    Task<AuthResult> RefreshAsync(string? rawRefreshToken);
    Task LogoutAsync(string? rawRefreshToken);
}

public sealed class AuthService(
    IUserRepository users,
    IRefreshTokenRepository tokens,
    IPasswordHasher<User> hasher,
    ITokenService tokenService,
    JwtOptions jwt,
    ILogger<AuthService> logger) : IAuthService
{
    // A fake user and hash. Used when the email is unknown, so the time taken looks the same as a wrong password.
    private static readonly User DummyUser = new("Dummy", "dummy@example.com");
    private static readonly string DummyHash =
        new PasswordHasher<User>().HashPassword(DummyUser, "dummy-password-never-used");

    public async Task<User> RegisterAsync(RegisterRequest request)
    {
        var user = new User(request.FullName, request.Email);   // trims + lower-cases the email

        if (await users.EmailExistsAsync(user.Email))
            throw new DomainConflictException("An account with this email already exists.");   // 409

        user.SetPasswordHash(hasher.HashPassword(user, request.Password));   // store the hash only
        await users.AddAsync(user);   // the unique index also catches two requests at the same moment

        logger.LogInformation("User {UserId} registered", user.Id);   // id only, never email or password
        return user;
    }

    public async Task<AuthResult> LoginAsync(LoginRequest request)
    {
        var user = await users.GetByEmailAsync(request.Email);

        // always run one password check (real or fake hash) so unknown email and wrong password look alike
        var hash = user?.PasswordHash ?? DummyHash;
        var outcome = hasher.VerifyHashedPassword(user ?? DummyUser, hash, request.Password);

        if (user is null || user.PasswordHash is null || outcome == PasswordVerificationResult.Failed)
        {
            // CHANGED 6.2: never the typed email. Our own user id when the account exists, so a guessing attack on one account is visible.
            if (user is null)
                logger.LogWarning("Login failed: no matching account");
            else
                logger.LogWarning("Login failed for user {UserId}", user.Id);
            throw new InvalidCredentialsException();   // same 401 message for both cases
        }

        var result = await IssueAsync(user, Guid.NewGuid());   // new login = new token family
        logger.LogInformation("Login succeeded for user {UserId}", user.Id);
        return result;
    }

    public async Task<AuthResult> RefreshAsync(string? rawRefreshToken)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawRefreshToken))
            throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException();

        var now = DateTime.UtcNow;
        var stored = await tokens.GetByHashAsync(tokenService.HashRefreshToken(rawRefreshToken))
            ?? throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException();

        // REUSE DETECTION: this token was already used (or revoked) -> someone replayed it
        if (stored.UsedAtUtc.HasValue || stored.RevokedAtUtc.HasValue)
        {
            await tokens.RevokeFamilyAsync(stored.FamilyId, now);   // kill every token of this login
            logger.LogWarning("Refresh token reuse detected for user {UserId}. Session {FamilyId} revoked",
                stored.UserId, stored.FamilyId);
            throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException();
        }

        if (stored.ExpiresAtUtc <= now)
            throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException();

        // read the user again, so the new access token reflects their CURRENT platform flag
        var user = await users.GetByIdAsync(stored.UserId)
            ?? throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException();

        var result = await IssueAsync(user, stored.FamilyId);   // ROTATION: new token, same family

        // mark the old one used. If another request got there first, this is a replay.
        if (!await tokens.TryMarkUsedAsync(stored.Id, result.RefreshTokenId, now))
        {
            await tokens.RevokeFamilyAsync(stored.FamilyId, now);
            logger.LogWarning("Refresh token reuse detected for user {UserId}. Session {FamilyId} revoked",
                stored.UserId, stored.FamilyId);
            throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException();
        }

        logger.LogInformation("Refresh token rotated for user {UserId}", user.Id);
        return result;
    }

    // Idempotent: unknown or missing token is fine, it just does nothing
    public async Task LogoutAsync(string? rawRefreshToken)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawRefreshToken)) return;

        var stored = await tokens.GetByHashAsync(tokenService.HashRefreshToken(rawRefreshToken));
        if (stored is null) return;

        await tokens.RevokeFamilyAsync(stored.FamilyId, DateTime.UtcNow);
        logger.LogInformation("User {UserId} logged out", stored.UserId);
    }

    // Makes the access token + a new refresh token (stored as a hash) for one family
    private async Task<AuthResult> IssueAsync(User user, Guid familyId)
    {
        var now = DateTime.UtcNow;
        var access = tokenService.CreateAccessToken(user.Id, user.IsPlatformAdmin);
        var (raw, hash) = tokenService.CreateRefreshToken();

        var refresh = new RefreshToken(user.Id, familyId, hash, now, now.AddDays(jwt.RefreshTokenDays));
        await tokens.AddAsync(refresh);

        return new AuthResult(access.Value, access.ExpiresAtUtc, raw, refresh.ExpiresAtUtc, refresh.Id);
    }
}

namespace RondiTrack.Auth;

// Settings for tokens. Everything except SigningKey comes from appsettings.json.
public sealed class JwtOptions
{
    public string Issuer { get; set; } = "RondiTrack";
    public string Audience { get; set; } = "RondiTrack.Api";
    public string SigningKey { get; set; } = "";      // comes ONLY from user-secrets (never appsettings)
    public int AccessTokenMinutes { get; set; } = 10; // short on purpose
    public int RefreshTokenDays { get; set; } = 7;
}
=== API/Auth/ProblemAuthorizationResultHandler.cs ===
using System.Text.Json;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization.Policy;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using RondiTrack.Telemetry;

namespace RondiTrack.Auth;

// Replaces the default empty 401/403 with the same problem+json shape as the rest of the API
public sealed class ProblemAuthorizationResultHandler(ILogger<ProblemAuthorizationResultHandler> logger)   // NEW 6.2: logger
    : IAuthorizationMiddlewareResultHandler
{
    public async Task HandleAsync(
        RequestDelegate next, HttpContext context, AuthorizationPolicy policy, PolicyAuthorizationResult authorizeResult)
    {
        if (authorizeResult.Challenged)   // no token, or a bad / expired token
        {
            context.Response.Headers.WWWAuthenticate = "Bearer";
            await WriteAsync(context, StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized, "Unauthorized",
                "A valid access token is required.");
            return;
        }

        if (authorizeResult.Forbidden)   // signed in, but not allowed
        {
            RondiMetrics.AuthorizationRefused(RefusalResults.Forbidden);   // NEW 6.2: count it
            await WriteAsync(context, StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden, "Forbidden",
                "You do not have permission to do this.");
            return;
        }

        await next(context);   // allowed
    }

    private async Task WriteAsync(HttpContext context, int status, string title, string detail)
    {
        var correlationId = CorrelationId.For(context);   // CHANGED 6.2: W3C trace id

        // NEW 6.2: 4xx at Information. Only our own user id (or "anonymous"), never a token, email or name.
        logger.LogInformation("Request rejected with status {Status} for user {UserId}. CorrelationId: {CorrelationId}",
            status, context.User.GetUserId()?.ToString() ?? "anonymous", correlationId);

        var problem = new ProblemDetails { Status = status, Title = title, Detail = detail };
        problem.Extensions["correlationId"] = correlationId;

        context.Response.StatusCode = status;
        context.Response.ContentType = "application/problem+json";
        await context.Response.WriteAsync(JsonSerializer.Serialize(problem));
    }
}
=== API/Auth/StokvelAccess.cs ===
using System.Security.Claims;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using RondiTrack.Telemetry;

namespace RondiTrack.Auth;

public interface IStokvelAccess
{
    Task EnsureAsync(ClaimsPrincipal user, Guid stokvelId, string policy, Guid? onBehalfOfUserId = null);
}

// One place that decides 404 vs 403. No try/catch, no role names.
public sealed class StokvelAccess(IAuthorizationService authorization, ILogger<StokvelAccess> logger) : IStokvelAccess   // NEW 6.2: logger
{
    public async Task EnsureAsync(ClaimsPrincipal user, Guid stokvelId, string policy, Guid? onBehalfOfUserId = null)
    {
        var result = await authorization.AuthorizeAsync(user, new StokvelResource(stokvelId, onBehalfOfUserId), policy);
        if (result.Succeeded) return;

        var notMember = result.Failure?.FailureReasons
            .Any(r => r.Message == StokvelAccessHandler.NotMemberReason) ?? false;

        // not a member: pretend the stokvel does not exist (same text as the real 404)
        if (notMember)
        {
            RondiMetrics.AuthorizationRefused(RefusalResults.NotFound);   // NEW 6.2: count it
            // NEW 6.2: Information. A refusal is an expected answer; a spike is caught by the metric. Our user id only.
            logger.LogInformation("Stokvel access refused ({Result}) for user {UserId}",
                RefusalResults.NotFound, user.GetUserId());
            throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Stokvel {stokvelId} was not found.");
        }

        // a member without the role already knows the stokvel exists
        RondiMetrics.AuthorizationRefused(RefusalResults.Forbidden);      // NEW 6.2: count it
        logger.LogInformation("Stokvel access refused ({Result}) for user {UserId}",
            RefusalResults.Forbidden, user.GetUserId());
        throw new ForbiddenException("You do not have the required role in this stokvel.");
    }
}
=== API/Auth/StokvelAuthorization.cs ===
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;

namespace RondiTrack.Auth;

// What level of access the endpoint needs
public enum StokvelAccessLevel
{
    Member,              // any member
    Officer,             // Treasurer or Admin of THAT stokvel
    RecordContribution   // own contribution, or officer for someone else's
}

// The custom requirement
public sealed record StokvelAccessRequirement(StokvelAccessLevel Level) : IAuthorizationRequirement;

// The resource being protected: one stokvel (and optionally who the action is for)
public sealed record StokvelResource(Guid StokvelId, Guid? OnBehalfOfUserId = null);

// Resource-based handler. It asks the DATABASE every time, so a removed or demoted member is refused at once.
public sealed class StokvelAccessHandler(IStokvelMemberRepository members)
    : AuthorizationHandler<StokvelAccessRequirement, StokvelResource>
{
    public const string NotMemberReason = "not-a-member";     // becomes 404
    public const string MissingRoleReason = "missing-role";   // becomes 403

    protected override async Task HandleRequirementAsync(
        AuthorizationHandlerContext context, StokvelAccessRequirement requirement, StokvelResource resource)
    {
        // platform admins (support staff) pass on every stokvel
        if (context.User.IsPlatformAdmin())
        {
            context.Succeed(requirement);
            return;
        }

        var callerId = context.User.GetUserId();
        if (callerId is null) return;   // not signed in: no success

        // the real check: the caller's membership row for THIS stokvel
        var membership = await members.GetAsync(callerId.Value, resource.StokvelId);
        if (membership is null)
        {
            context.Fail(new AuthorizationFailureReason(this, NotMemberReason));
            return;
        }

        var isOfficer = membership.Role is StokvelRole.Treasurer or StokvelRole.Admin;

        var needsOfficer = requirement.Level switch
        {
            StokvelAccessLevel.Officer => true,
            // recording for yourself is fine; for someone else you must be an officer
            StokvelAccessLevel.RecordContribution =>
                resource.OnBehalfOfUserId is Guid target && target != callerId.Value,
            _ => false
        };

        if (needsOfficer && !isOfficer)
        {
            context.Fail(new AuthorizationFailureReason(this, MissingRoleReason));
            return;
        }

        context.Succeed(requirement);
    }
}
=== API/Auth/TokenService.cs ===
using System.Security.Claims;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.WebUtilities;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.JsonWebTokens;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;

namespace RondiTrack.Auth;

public sealed record AccessToken(string Value, DateTime ExpiresAtUtc);

public interface ITokenService
{
    AccessToken CreateAccessToken(Guid userId, bool isPlatformAdmin);
    (string Raw, string Hash) CreateRefreshToken();
    string HashRefreshToken(string raw);
}

public sealed class TokenService(JwtOptions options) : ITokenService
{
    // the signing key as bytes (HS256 needs at least 32)
    private readonly SymmetricSecurityKey _key = new(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(options.SigningKey));

    public AccessToken CreateAccessToken(Guid userId, bool isPlatformAdmin)
    {
        var now = DateTime.UtcNow;
        var expires = now.AddMinutes(options.AccessTokenMinutes);

        // Only facts that can safely be stale for a few minutes. NO stokvel roles here.
        var claims = new List<Claim>
        {
            new("sub", userId.ToString()),          // who the user is (never changes)
            new("jti", Guid.NewGuid().ToString())   // unique id of this token
        };
        if (isPlatformAdmin)
            claims.Add(new Claim("role", AuthConstants.PlatformAdminRole));   // platform-level only

        var descriptor = new SecurityTokenDescriptor
        {
            Issuer = options.Issuer,
            Audience = options.Audience,
            Subject = new ClaimsIdentity(claims),
            IssuedAt = now,
            NotBefore = now,
            Expires = expires,
            SigningCredentials = new SigningCredentials(_key, SecurityAlgorithms.HmacSha256)
        };

        return new AccessToken(new JsonWebTokenHandler().CreateToken(descriptor), expires);
    }

    // Random bytes, NOT a JWT. The raw value goes to the client; only its hash is stored.
    public (string Raw, string Hash) CreateRefreshToken()
    {
        var raw = WebEncoders.Base64UrlEncode(RandomNumberGenerator.GetBytes(32));
        return (raw, HashRefreshToken(raw));
    }

    // SHA-256 is enough here because the token is 256 bits of randomness (not a guessable password)
    public string HashRefreshToken(string raw) =>
        Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(raw)));
}
=== API/Auth/UserAuthorization.cs ===
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;

namespace RondiTrack.Auth;

// "You may only touch your own account" (platform admins excepted)
public sealed record UserSelfRequirement : IAuthorizationRequirement;

public sealed record UserResource(Guid UserId);

public sealed class UserSelfHandler : AuthorizationHandler<UserSelfRequirement, UserResource>
{
    protected override Task HandleRequirementAsync(
        AuthorizationHandlerContext context, UserSelfRequirement requirement, UserResource resource)
    {
        // own account, or platform admin
        if (context.User.IsPlatformAdmin() || context.User.GetUserId() == resource.UserId)
            context.Succeed(requirement);

        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }
}
=== API/Auth/UserClaimsExtensions.cs ===
using System.Security.Claims;

namespace RondiTrack.Auth;

public static class UserClaimsExtensions
{
    // the signed-in user's id from the "sub" claim (null if there is none)
    public static Guid? GetUserId(this ClaimsPrincipal user) =>
        Guid.TryParse(user.FindFirstValue("sub"), out var id) ? id : null;

    // platform admin = has the platform role claim
    public static bool IsPlatformAdmin(this ClaimsPrincipal user) =>
        user.HasClaim("role", AuthConstants.PlatformAdminRole);
}

=== API/Common/ETagHelper.cs ===
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using RondiTrack.Api.Common.Paging;

namespace RondiTrack.Common;

// NEW: turns the xmin version number into an ETag header and back.
public static class ETagHelper
{
    // Puts the entity's version on the response as: ETag: "12345"
    public static void Set(HttpResponse response, uint version) =>
        response.Headers.ETag = $"\"{version}\"";

    // Reads the If-Match header. Missing -> 428, not a number -> 400.
    public static uint ParseIfMatch(string? header)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(header))
            throw new PreconditionRequiredException(
                "The If-Match header is required. Send the ETag you received from GET.");

        var text = header.Trim();
        if (text.StartsWith("W/")) text = text[2..];
        text = text.Trim('"');

        if (!uint.TryParse(text, out var version))
            throw new ApiBadRequestException("The If-Match header is not a valid ETag.");

        return version;
    }
}
=== API/Common/PageTokenCodec.cs ===
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;

namespace RondiTrack.Api.Common.Paging;

// CHANGED: LastId is now a Guid (your ids are Guids, not ints).
// Context = fingerprint of the sort + filters, so we can detect a token reused with different ones.
public record PageCursor(string Context, string LastValue, Guid LastId);

public static class PageTokenCodec
{
    // Turns the cursor into an opaque base64url string the client cannot read.
    public static string Encode(PageCursor cursor)
    {
        var json = JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(cursor);
        return Convert.ToBase64String(json).Replace('+', '-').Replace('/', '_').TrimEnd('=');
    }

    // Reads a token back. A broken token, or a token from a different sort/filter, gives a 400.
    public static PageCursor Decode(string token, string expectedContext)
    {
        PageCursor? cursor;
        try
        {
            var b64 = token.Replace('-', '+').Replace('_', '/');
            b64 = b64.PadRight(b64.Length + (4 - b64.Length % 4) % 4, '=');
            cursor = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<PageCursor>(Convert.FromBase64String(b64));
        }
        catch
        {
            throw new ApiBadRequestException("pageToken is invalid.");
        }

        if (cursor is null)
            throw new ApiBadRequestException("pageToken is invalid.");

        if (cursor.Context != expectedContext)
            throw new ApiBadRequestException(
                "pageToken was issued for a different sort or filter. Start again without a pageToken.");

        return cursor;
    }

    // Short fingerprint of sort + filters (same inputs always give the same text).
    public static string ContextOf(params object?[] parts)
    {
        var text = string.Join("|", parts.Select(p => p?.ToString() ?? ""));
        return Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(text)))[..12];
    }
}
=== API/Common/PagedResponse.cs ===
namespace RondiTrack.Api.Common.Paging;

// NEW: the shape every paged endpoint returns.
// NextPageToken is "" when there are no more results (end-of-results signal).
// There is no total count on purpose (see README).
public record PagedResponse<T>(IReadOnlyList<T> Items, string NextPageToken);
=== API/Common/PagingDefaults.cs ===
namespace RondiTrack.Api.Common.Paging;

public static class PagingDefaults
{
    public const int DefaultPageSize = 25;
    public const int MaxPageSize = 100;

    // NEW: page size rules.
    // null or 0  -> use the default
    // negative   -> 400
    // too big    -> quietly reduced to the maximum (not rejected)
    public static int Resolve(int? requested)
    {
        if (requested is null || requested == 0) return DefaultPageSize;
        if (requested < 0) throw new ApiBadRequestException("pageSize cannot be negative.");
        return Math.Min(requested.Value, MaxPageSize);
    }
}
=== API/Common/PreconditionRequiredException.cs ===
namespace RondiTrack.Common;

// NEW: thrown when an update arrives without the If-Match header. Becomes a 428 problem response.
public sealed class PreconditionRequiredException(string message) : Exception(message);
=== API/Controllers/AuthController.cs ===
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using RondiTrack.Auth;
using RondiTrack.Dtos;

namespace RondiTrack.Controllers;

[Route("api/auth")]
[AllowAnonymous]   // all four endpoints work without an access token
public class AuthController(IAuthService auth) : RondiControllerBase
{
    /// <summary>Registers a new account. Password must be at least 12 characters.</summary>
    [HttpPost("register")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(UserResponse), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<UserResponse>> Register(RegisterRequest request)
    {
        var user = await auth.RegisterAsync(request);
        return Created($"/api/users/{user.Id}", user.ToResponse());
    }

    /// <summary>Signs in. Returns the access token in the body and sets the refresh token as a cookie.</summary>
    [HttpPost("login")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(AuthResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<AuthResponse>> Login(LoginRequest request) =>
        TokenResponse(await auth.LoginAsync(request));

    /// <summary>Swaps the refresh cookie for a new access token and a new refresh cookie (rotation).</summary>
    [HttpPost("refresh")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(AuthResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<AuthResponse>> Refresh()
    {
        Request.Cookies.TryGetValue(AuthConstants.RefreshCookieName, out var raw);   // cookie is the only source
        return TokenResponse(await auth.RefreshAsync(raw));
    }

    /// <summary>Signs out: revokes the session's refresh tokens. Safe to call more than once.</summary>
    [HttpPost("logout")]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Logout()
    {
        Request.Cookies.TryGetValue(AuthConstants.RefreshCookieName, out var raw);
        await auth.LogoutAsync(raw);   // does nothing if there is no valid cookie
        Response.Cookies.Delete(AuthConstants.RefreshCookieName, CookieOptionsFor(null));   // same path as when it was set
        return NoContent();
    }

    // Sets the refresh cookie and returns ONLY the access token in the body
    private ActionResult<AuthResponse> TokenResponse(AuthResult result)
    {
        Response.Cookies.Append(AuthConstants.RefreshCookieName, result.RefreshToken,
            CookieOptionsFor(result.RefreshTokenExpiresAtUtc));
        Response.Headers.CacheControl = "no-store";   // tokens must never be cached
        return Ok(new AuthResponse(result.AccessToken, result.AccessTokenExpiresAtUtc, "Bearer"));
    }

    // The cookie flags (each one is justified in the README)
    private static CookieOptions CookieOptionsFor(DateTime? expiresUtc) => new()
    {
        HttpOnly = true,                              // JavaScript cannot read it, so XSS cannot steal it
        Secure = true,                                // only sent over HTTPS
        SameSite = SameSiteMode.Strict,               // never sent on cross-site requests (CSRF)
        Path = AuthConstants.RefreshCookiePath,       // only sent to /api/auth/*
        IsEssential = true,                           // not blocked by consent middleware
        Expires = expiresUtc.HasValue ? new DateTimeOffset(expiresUtc.Value) : null   // lives as long as the token
    };
}
=== API/Controllers/ContributionCyclesController.cs ===
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using RondiTrack.Api.Common.Paging;
using RondiTrack.Auth;
using RondiTrack.Common;
using RondiTrack.Data;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Dtos;
using RondiTrack.Services;

namespace RondiTrack.Controllers;

[Route("api/stokvels/{stokvelId:guid}/cycles")]
[ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized)]   // NEW 6.1
[ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden)]      // NEW 6.1
public class ContributionCyclesController(
    IContributionCycleRepository cycles,
    IStokvelRepository stokvels,
    ContributionPagingService contributionPaging) : RondiControllerBase   // CHANGED 5.3: paging service replaces the strategy switch
{
    /// <summary>Lists a stokvel's contribution cycles.</summary>
    [HttpGet]
    [RequireStokvelMember]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyList<ContributionCycleResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<IReadOnlyList<ContributionCycleResponse>>> GetAll(Guid stokvelId)
    {
        if (await stokvels.GetByIdAsync(stokvelId) is null)
            throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Stokvel {stokvelId} was not found.");

        var all = await cycles.GetByStokvelAsync(stokvelId);
        return Ok(all.Select(c => c.ToResponse()));
    }

    /// <summary>Gets a single contribution cycle. The ETag header is the concurrency token.</summary>
    [HttpGet("{id:guid}")]
    [RequireStokvelMember]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ContributionCycleResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ContributionCycleResponse>> GetById(Guid stokvelId, Guid id)
    {
        var cycle = await cycles.GetByIdAsync(id);
        if (cycle is null || cycle.StokvelId != stokvelId)
            throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Cycle {id} was not found.");

        ETagHelper.Set(Response, cycle.Version);   // ADDED 5.3: token leaves the server
        return Ok(cycle.ToResponse());
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Lists the contributions recorded for a cycle, with each member's details.
    /// CHANGED 5.3: paged, sortable and filterable. All of it runs in the database.
    /// Query: pageSize, pageToken, sort (date|-date|amount|-amount), userId, dateFrom, dateTo.
    /// </summary>
    [HttpGet("{cycleId:guid}/contributions")]
    [RequireStokvelMember]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(PagedResponse<ContributionDetailResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<ContributionDetailResponse>>> GetContributions(
        Guid stokvelId, Guid cycleId, [FromQuery] ContributionListQuery query, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var cycle = await cycles.GetByIdAsync(cycleId);
        if (cycle is null || cycle.StokvelId != stokvelId)
            throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Cycle {cycleId} was not found.");

        return Ok(await contributionPaging.ListAsync(cycleId, query, ct));
    }

    /// <summary>Opens a new contribution cycle. Treasurer or Admin only.</summary>
    [HttpPost]
    [RequireStokvelOfficer]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ContributionCycleResponse), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ContributionCycleResponse>> Create(Guid stokvelId, ContributionCycleRequest request)
    {
        if (await stokvels.GetByIdAsync(stokvelId) is null)
            throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Stokvel {stokvelId} was not found.");

        var cycle = new ContributionCycle(stokvelId, request.CycleNumber, request.TargetAmount);
        await cycles.AddAsync(cycle);

        ETagHelper.Set(Response, cycle.Version);   // ADDED 5.3
        return CreatedAtAction(nameof(GetById), new { stokvelId, id = cycle.Id }, cycle.ToResponse());
    }

    /// <summary>Updates a cycle. Treasurer or Admin only. Requires the If-Match header (ETag from GET).</summary>
    [HttpPut("{id:guid}")]
    [RequireStokvelOfficer]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ContributionCycleResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status412PreconditionFailed)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status428PreconditionRequired)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ContributionCycleResponse>> Update(
        Guid stokvelId, Guid id, ContributionCycleRequest request,
        [FromHeader(Name = "If-Match")] string? ifMatch)
    {
        var expectedVersion = ETagHelper.ParseIfMatch(ifMatch);   // ADDED 5.3: the token is required

        var cycle = await cycles.GetByIdAsync(id);
        if (cycle is null || cycle.StokvelId != stokvelId)
            throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Cycle {id} was not found.");

        cycle.UpdateDetails(request.CycleNumber, request.TargetAmount);
        cycle.SetExpectedVersion(expectedVersion);   // ADDED 5.3: use the version the CLIENT saw, not the one just loaded
        await cycles.UpdateAsync(cycle);

        ETagHelper.Set(Response, cycle.Version);     // ADDED 5.3: hand back the new token
        return Ok(cycle.ToResponse());
    }

    /// <summary>Deletes a contribution cycle. Treasurer or Admin only.</summary>
    [HttpDelete("{id:guid}")]
    [RequireStokvelOfficer]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(Guid stokvelId, Guid id)
    {
        var cycle = await cycles.GetByIdAsync(id);
        if (cycle is null || cycle.StokvelId != stokvelId)
            throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Cycle {id} was not found.");

        await cycles.DeleteAsync(id);
        return NoContent();
    }
}

=== API/Controllers/StokvelsController.cs ===
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using RondiTrack.Api.Common.Paging;
using RondiTrack.Auth;
using RondiTrack.Common;
using RondiTrack.Data;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Dtos;
using RondiTrack.Services;

namespace RondiTrack.Controllers;

[Route("api/stokvels")]
[ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized)]   // NEW 6.1: documented on every action
[ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden)]      // NEW 6.1
public class StokvelsController(
    IStokvelRepository stokvels,
    IStokvelMembershipService membershipService,
    IContributionService contributionService,
    MemberPagingService memberPaging,
    IStokvelAccess access) : RondiControllerBase   // NEW 6.1: access = the 404/403 decision for the contribution check
{
    /// <summary>Lists all stokvels. Platform admins only.</summary>
    [HttpGet]
    [Authorize(Roles = AuthConstants.PlatformAdminRole)]   // NEW 6.1: the ONE plain role check in the whole API
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyList<StokvelResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<IReadOnlyList<StokvelResponse>>> GetAll()
    {
        var all = await stokvels.GetAllAsync();
        return Ok(all.Select(s => s.ToResponse()));
    }

    /// <summary>Gets a single stokvel by id. The ETag header is the concurrency token.</summary>
    [HttpGet("{stokvelId:guid}")]
    [RequireStokvelMember]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(StokvelResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<StokvelResponse>> GetById(Guid stokvelId)
    {
        var stokvel = await stokvels.GetByIdAsync(stokvelId) ?? throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Stokvel {stokvelId} was not found.");
        ETagHelper.Set(Response, stokvel.Version);   // ADDED 5.3: token leaves the server
        return Ok(stokvel.ToResponse());
    }

    /// <summary>Creates a new stokvel. Any signed-in user (see README gap about the creator).</summary>
    [HttpPost]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(StokvelResponse), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<StokvelResponse>> Create(StokvelRequest request)
    {
        var stokvel = new Stokvel(request.Name, request.ContributionAmount, request.Frequency, request.MaxMembers);
        await stokvels.AddAsync(stokvel);

        ETagHelper.Set(Response, stokvel.Version);   // ADDED 5.3
        return CreatedAtAction(nameof(GetById), new { stokvelId = stokvel.Id }, stokvel.ToResponse());
    }

    /// <summary>Updates a stokvel's details. Treasurer or Admin only. Requires the If-Match header.</summary>
    [HttpPut("{stokvelId:guid}")]
    [RequireStokvelOfficer]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(StokvelResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status412PreconditionFailed)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status428PreconditionRequired)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<StokvelResponse>> Update(
        Guid stokvelId, StokvelRequest request, [FromHeader(Name = "If-Match")] string? ifMatch)
    {
        var expectedVersion = ETagHelper.ParseIfMatch(ifMatch);   // ADDED 5.3: the token is required

        var stokvel = await stokvels.GetByIdAsync(stokvelId) ?? throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Stokvel {stokvelId} was not found.");
        stokvel.UpdateDetails(request.Name, request.ContributionAmount, request.Frequency, request.MaxMembers);
        stokvel.SetExpectedVersion(expectedVersion);   // ADDED 5.3: use the version the CLIENT saw
        await stokvels.UpdateAsync(stokvel);

        ETagHelper.Set(Response, stokvel.Version);     // ADDED 5.3: hand back the new token
        return Ok(stokvel.ToResponse());
    }

    /// <summary>Deletes a stokvel. Treasurer or Admin only.</summary>
    [HttpDelete("{stokvelId:guid}")]
    [RequireStokvelOfficer]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(Guid stokvelId)
    {
        if (!await stokvels.DeleteAsync(stokvelId))
            throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Stokvel {stokvelId} was not found.");
        return NoContent();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Lists a stokvel's members in rotation order. CHANGED 5.3: paged in the database.
    /// Query: pageSize, pageToken.
    /// </summary>
    [HttpGet("{stokvelId:guid}/members")]
    [RequireStokvelMember]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(PagedResponse<UserResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<UserResponse>>> GetMembers(
        Guid stokvelId, [FromQuery] int? pageSize, [FromQuery] string? pageToken, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var result = await memberPaging.ListAsync(stokvelId, pageSize, pageToken, ct)
            ?? throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Stokvel {stokvelId} was not found.");
        return Ok(result);
    }

    /// <summary>Gets a single member of a stokvel.</summary>
    [HttpGet("{stokvelId:guid}/members/{userId:guid}")]
    [RequireStokvelMember]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(UserResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<UserResponse>> GetMember(Guid stokvelId, Guid userId)
    {
        var stokvel = await stokvels.GetByIdAsync(stokvelId) ?? throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Stokvel {stokvelId} was not found.");
        var member = stokvel.FindMember(userId) ?? throw new DomainNotFoundException($"User {userId} was not found in this stokvel.");
        return Ok(member.ToResponse());
    }

    /// <summary>Adds an existing user as a member. Treasurer or Admin only.</summary>
    [HttpPost("{stokvelId:guid}/members")]
    [RequireStokvelOfficer]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(UserResponse), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status422UnprocessableEntity)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<UserResponse>> AddMember(Guid stokvelId, AddMemberRequest request)
    {
        var user = await membershipService.AddMemberAsync(stokvelId, request.UserId);
        return CreatedAtAction(nameof(GetMember), new { stokvelId, userId = user.Id }, user.ToResponse());
    }

    /// <summary>Removes a member from a stokvel. Treasurer or Admin only.</summary>
    [HttpDelete("{stokvelId:guid}/members/{userId:guid}")]
    [RequireStokvelOfficer]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> RemoveMember(Guid stokvelId, Guid userId)
    {
        await membershipService.RemoveMemberAsync(stokvelId, userId);
        return NoContent();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Records a contribution. A member may record their own; a Treasurer or Admin may record for anyone.
    /// Requires an Idempotency-Key header.
    /// </summary>
    [HttpPost("{stokvelId:guid}/contributions")]
    [RequireStokvelMember]   // NEW 6.1: must at least be a member (non-members get 404 before anything else)
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ContributionResponse), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status422UnprocessableEntity)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<ContributionResponse>> RecordContribution(
        Guid stokvelId, ContributionRequest request, [FromHeader(Name = "Idempotency-Key")] string? idempotencyKey)
    {
        // NEW 6.1: own contribution = ok, someone else's = officer only (403 otherwise)
        await access.EnsureAsync(User, stokvelId, Policies.RecordContribution, request.UserId);

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(idempotencyKey))
            throw new DomainValidationException("The Idempotency-Key header is required.");

        var (response, _) = await contributionService.RecordContributionAsync(stokvelId, request, idempotencyKey);
        return StatusCode(StatusCodes.Status201Created, response);
    }
}
=== API/Controllers/UsersController.cs ===
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using RondiTrack.Auth;
using RondiTrack.Common;
using RondiTrack.Data;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Dtos;

namespace RondiTrack.Controllers;

[Route("api/users")]
[ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized)]   // NEW 6.1
[ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden)]      // NEW 6.1
public class UsersController(IUserRepository users, IStokvelRepository stokvels) : RondiControllerBase
{
    /// <summary>Lists all users. Platform admins only.</summary>
    [HttpGet]
    [Authorize(Policy = Policies.PlatformAdmin)]   // NEW 6.1: nobody else may list everyone's emails
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IReadOnlyList<UserResponse>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<IReadOnlyList<UserResponse>>> GetAll()
    {
        var all = await users.GetAllAsync();
        return Ok(all.Select(u => u.ToResponse()));
    }

    /// <summary>Gets a user by id (your own account, or platform admin). The ETag header is the concurrency token.</summary>
    [HttpGet("{id:guid}")]
    [RequireUserSelf]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(UserResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<UserResponse>> GetById(Guid id)
    {
        var user = await users.GetByIdAsync(id) ?? throw new DomainNotFoundException($"User {id} was not found.");
        ETagHelper.Set(Response, user.Version);   // ADDED 5.3: token leaves the server
        return Ok(user.ToResponse());
    }

    /// <summary>Creates a user without a password. Platform admins only (people sign up with /api/auth/register).</summary>
    [HttpPost]
    [Authorize(Policy = Policies.PlatformAdmin)]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(UserResponse), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<UserResponse>> Create(UserRequest request)
    {
        var user = new User(request.FullName, request.Email);

        if (await users.EmailExistsAsync(user.Email))
            throw new DomainConflictException("A user with this email already exists.");   // CHANGED 6.2: no email in the response

        await users.AddAsync(user);

        ETagHelper.Set(Response, user.Version);   // ADDED 5.3
        return CreatedAtAction(nameof(GetById), new { id = user.Id }, user.ToResponse());
    }

    /// <summary>Updates a user's name and email (your own account, or platform admin). Requires If-Match.</summary>
    [HttpPut("{id:guid}")]
    [RequireUserSelf]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(UserResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status412PreconditionFailed)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status428PreconditionRequired)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<UserResponse>> Update(
        Guid id, UserRequest request, [FromHeader(Name = "If-Match")] string? ifMatch)
    {
        var expectedVersion = ETagHelper.ParseIfMatch(ifMatch);   // ADDED 5.3: the token is required

        var user = await users.GetByIdAsync(id) ?? throw new DomainNotFoundException($"User {id} was not found.");

        if (await users.EmailExistsAsync(request.Email, excludingUserId: id))
            throw new DomainConflictException("A user with this email already exists.");   // CHANGED 6.2: no email in the response

        user.UpdateDetails(request.FullName, request.Email);
        user.SetExpectedVersion(expectedVersion);   // ADDED 5.3: use the version the CLIENT saw
        await users.UpdateAsync(user);

        ETagHelper.Set(Response, user.Version);     // ADDED 5.3: hand back the new token
        return Ok(user.ToResponse());
    }

    /// <summary>Deletes a user (your own account, or platform admin). Fails if the user still belongs to any stokvel.</summary>
    [HttpDelete("{id:guid}")]
    [RequireUserSelf]   // NEW 6.1
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ProblemDetails), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(Guid id)
    {
        if (await users.GetByIdAsync(id) is null)
            throw new DomainNotFoundException($"User {id} was not found.");

        if (await stokvels.AnyWithMemberAsync(id))
            throw new DomainConflictException("This user belongs to at least one stokvel. Remove them from all stokvels first.");

        await users.DeleteAsync(id);
        return NoContent();
    }
}

            contribution.CycleId, contribution.Amount, contribution.RecordedAtUtc);

=== API/Errors/RondiExceptionHandler.cs ===
using FluentValidation;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Diagnostics;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Npgsql;
using RondiTrack.Api.Common.Paging;
using RondiTrack.Auth;
using RondiTrack.Common;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Telemetry;
// using RondiTrack.Web;   // TODO 6.3 (Task 2): uncomment once CorsSetup exists
using System.Text.Json;

namespace RondiTrack.Errors;

// This class catches every exception that escapes a controller or service.
// Its job: turn the exception into a safe, consistent problem+json response.
// (Registered in Program.cs with AddExceptionHandler and UseExceptionHandler.)
public sealed class RondiExceptionHandler(ILogger<RondiExceptionHandler> logger) : IExceptionHandler
{
    public async ValueTask<bool> TryHandleAsync(HttpContext httpContext, Exception exception, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        // TODO 6.3 (Task 2): uncomment this line after adding CorsSetup.
        // UseExceptionHandler wipes response headers (including CORS ones), so this puts them back.
        // await CorsSetup.ReapplyAsync(httpContext);

        // One id that links the response, the log line and the trace (from 6.2)
        var correlationId = CorrelationId.For(httpContext);   // CHANGED 6.2: the W3C trace id, not TraceIdentifier

        // Default = an unknown error, so a 500 with a generic message.
        // Any exception we do not recognise below ends up here.
        var status = StatusCodes.Status500InternalServerError;
        var title = "Internal Server Error";
        var detail = "An unexpected error occurred.";   // the ONLY text a client sees for a 5xx

        // Below: pick the status, title and detail from the exception type.
        // Only our own exception types pass on their message, because we wrote those messages.

        // FluentValidation failed -> 400
        if (exception is ValidationException vex)
        {
            status = StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest;
            title = "Validation Failed";
            detail = string.Join(" ", vex.Errors.Select(e => e.ErrorMessage));
        }
        // Something does not exist -> 404
        else if (exception is DomainNotFoundException)
        {
            status = StatusCodes.Status404NotFound;
            title = "Not Found";
            detail = exception.Message;
        }
        // A business rule clash (duplicate, wrong state) -> 409
        else if (exception is DomainConflictException)
        {
            status = StatusCodes.Status409Conflict;
            title = "Conflict";
            detail = exception.Message;
        }
        // The request points at something that is not there (bad reference) -> 422
        else if (exception is DomainReferenceException)
        {
            status = StatusCodes.Status422UnprocessableEntity;
            title = "Unprocessable Entity";
            detail = exception.Message;
        }
        // A business rule says the input is invalid -> 400
        else if (exception is DomainValidationException)
        {
            status = StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest;
            title = "Bad Request";
            detail = exception.Message;
        }
        // ADDED 5.3: bad paging / sort / filter / token input -> 400
        else if (exception is ApiBadRequestException)
        {
            status = StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest;
            title = "Bad Request";
            detail = exception.Message;
        }
        // ADDED 5.3: update sent without If-Match -> 428
        else if (exception is PreconditionRequiredException)
        {
            status = StatusCodes.Status428PreconditionRequired;
            title = "Precondition Required";
            detail = exception.Message;
        }
        // NEW 6.1: wrong login or bad refresh token -> 401 (the message is the same for every cause)
        else if (exception is InvalidCredentialsException or InvalidRefreshTokenException)
        {
            status = StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized;
            title = "Unauthorized";
            detail = exception.Message;
            httpContext.Response.Headers.WWWAuthenticate = "Bearer";   // tells the client which scheme to use
        }
        // NEW 6.1: signed in but missing the role in this stokvel -> 403
        else if (exception is ForbiddenException)
        {
            status = StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden;
            title = "Forbidden";
            detail = exception.Message;
        }
        // ADDED 5.3: xmin token was stale (someone else changed the row first).
        // If the client sent If-Match, the precondition failed (412).
        // If there was no If-Match (a race inside the server, e.g. two payouts at once), it is a plain 409.
        else if (exception is DbUpdateConcurrencyException)
        {
            if (httpContext.Request.Headers.ContainsKey("If-Match"))
            {
                status = StatusCodes.Status412PreconditionFailed;
                title = "Precondition Failed";
                detail = "This record was changed by someone else since you loaded it. Reload it and try again.";
            }
            else
            {
                status = StatusCodes.Status409Conflict;
                title = "Conflict";
                detail = "This record was changed by someone else at the same time. Reload it and try again.";
            }
        }
        // ADDED 5.3: a database unique constraint fired (SQLSTATE 23505) -> 409 instead of 500.
        // We write our own detail, so no database text reaches the client.
        else if (exception is DbUpdateException { InnerException: PostgresException { SqlState: PostgresErrorCodes.UniqueViolation } })
        {
            status = StatusCodes.Status409Conflict;
            title = "Conflict";
            detail = "That record already exists (it would break a uniqueness rule).";
        }

        // ---------- Logging ----------
        // CHANGED 6.2 (POPIA): a 5xx is a real failure, so it gets the full exception at Error.
        if (status >=
=== API/GlobalUsings.cs ===

=== API/Services/ContributionPagingService.cs ===
using System.Globalization;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using RondiTrack.Api.Common.Paging;
using RondiTrack.Data;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Dtos;

namespace RondiTrack.Services;

// CHANGED: rewritten for your real names (RondiTrackDbContext, Guid ids, RecordedAtUtc).
// The whole query is built as IQueryable, so WHERE / ORDER BY / LIMIT run inside PostgreSQL.
public sealed class ContributionPagingService(RondiTrackDbContext db)
{
    // The ONLY sorts a client may ask for. Anything else is a 400.
    private static readonly string[] AllowedSorts = ["date", "-date", "amount", "-amount"];

    public async Task<PagedResponse<ContributionDetailResponse>> ListAsync(
        Guid cycleId, ContributionListQuery q, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var pageSize = PagingDefaults.Resolve(q.PageSize);

        var sort = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(q.Sort) ? "date" : q.Sort.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        if (!AllowedSorts.Contains(sort))
            throw new ApiBadRequestException(
                $"Unknown sort '{q.Sort}'. Allowed: {string.Join(", ", AllowedSorts)}.");

        var from = ToUtc(q.DateFrom);
        var to = ToUtc(q.DateTo);
        if (from.HasValue && to.HasValue && from > to)
            throw new ApiBadRequestException("dateFrom cannot be after dateTo.");

        IQueryable<Contribution> query = db.Contributions
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(c => c.CycleId == cycleId);

        if (q.UserId.HasValue)
        {
            var uid = q.UserId.Value;
            query = query.Where(c => c.UserId == uid);
        }
        if (from.HasValue)
        {
            var f = from.Value;
            query = query.Where(c => c.RecordedAtUtc >= f);
        }
        if (to.HasValue)
        {
            var t = to.Value;
            query = query.Where(c => c.RecordedAtUtc <= t);
        }

        // Fingerprint of sort + filters, stored inside every token we hand out.
        var context = PageTokenCodec.ContextOf(
            sort, cycleId, q.UserId, from?.ToString("O"), to?.ToString("O"));

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(q.PageToken))
        {
            var cursor = PageTokenCodec.Decode(q.PageToken, context);
            query = ApplyAfterCursor(query, sort, cursor);   // keyset: "rows after the last one you saw"
        }

        query = ApplyOrder(query, sort);

        // Ask for ONE extra row. If it comes back, there is a next page.
        var rows = await query
            .Take(pageSize + 1)
            .Select(c => new ContributionDetailResponse(
                c.Id, c.UserId, c.Member.User.FullName, c.Member.User.Email,
                c.Member.Role, c.Member.RotationPosition, c.Amount, c.RecordedAtUtc))
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        var hasMore = rows.Count > pageSize;
        var page = rows.Take(pageSize).ToList();

        var next = "";
        if (hasMore)
        {
            var last = page[^1];
            var lastValue = sort.EndsWith("date")
                ? last.RecordedAtUtc.ToString("O", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)
                : last.Amount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            next = PageTokenCodec.Encode(new PageCursor(context, lastValue, last.Id));
        }

        return new PagedResponse<ContributionDetailResponse>(page, next);
    }

    // Every sort ends with Id, so rows with equal date/amount always come in the same order.
    private static IQueryable<Contribution> ApplyOrder(IQueryable<Contribution> q, string sort) => sort switch
    {
        "date"   => q.OrderBy(c => c.RecordedAtUtc).ThenBy(c => c.Id),
        "-date"  => q.OrderByDescending(c => c.RecordedAtUtc).ThenByDescending(c => c.Id),
        "amount" => q.OrderBy(c => c.Amount).ThenBy(c => c.Id),
        _        => q.OrderByDescending(c => c.Amount).ThenByDescending(c => c.Id),
    };

    // Keyset condition: rows that come AFTER (lastValue, lastId) in the chosen order.
    private static IQueryable<Contribution> ApplyAfterCursor(
        IQueryable<Contribution> q, string sort, PageCursor cur)
    {
        var lastId = cur.LastId;

        if (sort.EndsWith("date"))
        {
            if (!DateTime.TryParse(cur.LastValue, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                    DateTimeStyles.RoundtripKind, out var parsed))
                throw new ApiBadRequestException("pageToken is invalid.");

            var d = DateTime.SpecifyKind(parsed, DateTimeKind.Utc);
            return sort == "date"
                ? q.Where(c => c.RecordedAtUtc > d || (c.RecordedAtUtc == d && c.Id.CompareTo(lastId) > 0))
                : q.Where(c => c.RecordedAtUtc < d || (c.RecordedAtUtc == d && c.Id.CompareTo(lastId) < 0));
        }

        if (!decimal.TryParse(cur.LastValue, NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var a))
            throw new ApiBadRequestException("pageToken is invalid.");

        return sort == "amount"
            ? q.Where(c => c.Amount > a || (c.Amount == a && c.Id.CompareTo(lastId) > 0))
            : q.Where(c => c.Amount < a || (c.Amount == a && c.Id.CompareTo(lastId) < 0));
    }

    // npgsql only accepts UTC for timestamptz, so a date typed by a client is treated as UTC.
    private static DateTime? ToUtc(DateTime? value) => value is null ? null
        : value.Value.Kind == DateTimeKind.Utc ? value
        : value.Value.Kind == DateTimeKind.Local ? value.Value.ToUniversalTime()
        : DateTime.SpecifyKind(value.Value, DateTimeKind.Utc);
}
=== API/Services/ContributionQueries.cs ===
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Dtos;

namespace RondiTrack.Data;

// The naive N+1 version and the two fixes, side by side
public interface IContributionQueries
{
    Task<IReadOnlyList<ContributionDetailResponse>> GetForCycleNaiveAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid cycleId);
    Task<IReadOnlyList<ContributionDetailResponse>> GetForCycleIncludeAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid cycleId);
    Task<IReadOnlyList<ContributionDetailResponse>> GetForCycleProjectedAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid cycleId);
}

public sealed class ContributionQueries(RondiTrackDbContext db) : IContributionQueries
{
    // VERSION 1: deliberately NAIVE. 1 query for the rows, then 2 more queries PER ROW.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<ContributionDetailResponse>> GetForCycleNaiveAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid cycleId)
    {
        var rows = await db.Contributions
            .Where(c => c.StokvelId == stokvelId && c.CycleId == cycleId)
            .OrderBy(c => c.RecordedAtUtc)
            .ToListAsync();

        var result = new List<ContributionDetailResponse>();
        foreach (var c in rows)
        {
            await db.Entry(c).Reference(x => x.Member).LoadAsync();        // one round trip per row
            await db.Entry(c.Member).Reference(m => m.User).LoadAsync();   // another round trip per row
            result.Add(ToDto(c));
        }
        return result;
    }

    // VERSION 2: FIX with eager loading. One query with joins, pulls the whole graph.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<ContributionDetailResponse>> GetForCycleIncludeAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid cycleId)
    {
        var rows = await db.Contributions
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(c => c.StokvelId == stokvelId && c.CycleId == cycleId)
            .Include(c => c.Member).ThenInclude(m => m.User)
            .OrderBy(c => c.RecordedAtUtc)
            .ToListAsync();

        return rows.Select(ToDto).ToList();
    }

    // VERSION 3: FIX with projection. One query, only the columns the response needs. THIS ONE SHIPS.
    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<ContributionDetailResponse>> GetForCycleProjectedAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid cycleId) =>
        await db.Contributions
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(c => c.StokvelId == stokvelId && c.CycleId == cycleId)
            .OrderBy(c => c.RecordedAtUtc)
            .Select(c => new ContributionDetailResponse(
                c.Id, c.UserId, c.Member.User.FullName, c.Member.User.Email,
                c.Member.Role, c.Member.RotationPosition, c.Amount, c.RecordedAtUtc))
            .ToListAsync();

    private static ContributionDetailResponse ToDto(Contribution c) =>
        new(c.Id, c.UserId, c.Member.User.FullName, c.Member.User.Email,
            c.Member.Role, c.Member.RotationPosition, c.Amount, c.RecordedAtUtc);
}
=== API/Services/ContributionServices.cs ===
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using RondiTrack.Data;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Dtos;
using RondiTrack.Telemetry;   // NEW 6.2

namespace RondiTrack.Services;

public interface IContributionService
{
    Task<(ContributionResponse Response, bool WasReplayed)> RecordContributionAsync(
        Guid stokvelId, ContributionRequest request, string idempotencyKey);
}

public sealed class ContributionService(
    IStokvelRepository stokvels,
    IUserRepository users,
    IContributionCycleRepository cycles,
    IContributionRepository contributions,
    IIdempotencyStore idempotencyStore) : IContributionService
{
    public async Task<(ContributionResponse Response, bool WasReplayed)> RecordContributionAsync(
        Guid stokvelId, ContributionRequest request, string idempotencyKey)
    {
        var requestHash = ComputeHash(stokvelId, request);

        var existing = await idempotencyStore.GetAsync(idempotencyKey);
        if (existing is not null)
        {
            if (existing.RequestHash != requestHash)
                throw new DomainConflictException("This Idempotency-Key was already used with a different request body.");

            return (existing.Response, true);
        }

        var stokvel = await stokvels.GetByIdAsync(stokvelId)
            ?? throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Stokvel {stokvelId} was not found.");

        var user = await users.GetByIdAsync(request.UserId)
            ?? throw new DomainReferenceException($"User {request.UserId} does not exist.");

        if (!stokvel.HasMember(user.Id))
            throw new DomainReferenceException("That user is not a member of this stokvel.");   // CHANGED 6.2: no name, no stokvel name

        var cycle = await cycles.GetByIdAsync(request.CycleId);
        if (cycle is null || cycle.StokvelId != stokvelId)
            throw new DomainReferenceException($"Cycle {request.CycleId} does not exist for this stokvel.");

        if (await contributions.ExistsAsync(stokvelId, user.Id, request.CycleId))
            throw new DomainConflictException("This member has already contributed for this cycle.");   // CHANGED 6.2: no name

        var contribution = new Contribution(stokvelId, user.Id, request.CycleId, request.Amount);
        await contributions.AddAsync(contribution);
        RondiMetrics.ContributionRecorded();   // NEW 6.2: counted only after the row is really saved

        var response = contribution.ToResponse();
        await idempotencyStore.SaveAsync(idempotencyKey, new IdempotencyRecord(requestHash, response));

        return (response, false);
    }

    private static string ComputeHash(Guid stokvelId, ContributionRequest request)
    {
        var raw = $"{stokvelId}|{request.UserId}|{request.CycleId}|{request.Amount}";
        var bytes = SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(raw));
        return Convert.ToHexString(bytes);
    }
}
=== API/Services/IdempotencyStore.cs ===
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using RondiTrack.Dtos;

namespace RondiTrack.Data;

public sealed record IdempotencyRecord(string RequestHash, ContributionResponse Response);

public interface IIdempotencyStore
{
    Task<IdempotencyRecord?> GetAsync(string key);
    Task SaveAsync(string key, IdempotencyRecord record);
}

public sealed class InMemoryIdempotencyStore : IIdempotencyStore
{
    private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, IdempotencyRecord> _records = new();

    public Task<IdempotencyRecord?> GetAsync(string key)
    {
        _records.TryGetValue(key, out var record);
        return Task.FromResult(record);
    }

    public Task SaveAsync(string key, IdempotencyRecord record)
    {
        _records[key] = record;
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }
}
=== API/Services/MemberPagingService.cs ===
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using RondiTrack.Api.Common.Paging;
using RondiTrack.Data;
using RondiTrack.Dtos;

namespace RondiTrack.Services;

// NEW: second paged endpoint. Members of a stokvel, in rotation order.
// RotationPosition is unique per stokvel (unique index from 5.2), so it is its own tiebreaker.
public sealed class MemberPagingService(RondiTrackDbContext db)
{
    // Returns null when the stokvel does not exist (controller turns that into 404).
    public async Task<PagedResponse<UserResponse>?> ListAsync(
        Guid stokvelId, int? pageSizeRequested, string? pageToken, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var pageSize = PagingDefaults.Resolve(pageSizeRequested);

        if (!await db.Stokvels.AnyAsync(s => s.Id == stokvelId, ct)) return null;

        var context = PageTokenCodec.ContextOf("rotation", stokvelId);

        var query = db.StokvelMembers.AsNoTracking().Where(m => m.StokvelId == stokvelId);

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pageToken))
        {
            var cursor = PageTokenCodec.Decode(pageToken, context);
            if (!int.TryParse(cursor.LastValue, out var lastPosition))
                throw new ApiBadRequestException("pageToken is invalid.");
            query = query.Where(m => m.RotationPosition > lastPosition);   // keyset
        }

        var rows = await query
            .OrderBy(m => m.RotationPosition)
            .Take(pageSize + 1)
            .Select(m => new
            {
                m.RotationPosition,
                m.User.Id,
                m.User.FullName,
                m.User.Email,
                m.User.CreatedAtUtc
            })
            .ToListAsync(ct);

        var hasMore = rows.Count > pageSize;
        var page = rows.Take(pageSize).ToList();

        var next = hasMore
            ? PageTokenCodec.Encode(new PageCursor(context, page[^1].RotationPosition.ToString(), Guid.Empty))
            : "";

        var items = page.Select(r => new UserResponse(r.Id, r.FullName, r.Email, r.CreatedAtUtc)).ToList();
        return new PagedResponse<UserResponse>(items, next);
    }
}
=== API/Services/PayoutServices.cs ===
using System.Diagnostics;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using RondiTrack.Data;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Dtos;
using RondiTrack.Telemetry;

namespace RondiTrack.Services;

// Lets a test force a failure between the two writes. Does nothing in production.
public interface IPayoutFaultHook
{
    void AfterPayoutInserted();
}

public sealed class NoOpPayoutFaultHook : IPayoutFaultHook
{
    public void AfterPayoutInserted() { }
}

public interface IPayoutService
{
    Task<PayoutResponse> ProcessNextPayoutAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid cycleId, CancellationToken ct = default);
}

// EDIT 5.2: now also receives IStokvelMemberRepository (members are no longer read straight from the DbContext)
public sealed class PayoutService(
    RondiTrackDbContext db,
    IPayoutFaultHook faultHook,
    IStokvelMemberRepository members) : IPayoutService
{
    // NEW 6.2: the public method only adds the span and the metric. The money logic is unchanged (below).
    public async Task<PayoutResponse> ProcessNextPayoutAsync(
        Guid stokvelId, Guid cycleId, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        // our own span, named for the business operation. The SQL spans start INSIDE it, so they nest under it.
        using var activity = RondiTelemetry.Source.StartActivity("payout.process");

        try
        {
            var result = await ProcessAsync(stokvelId, cycleId, activity, ct);

            activity?.SetTag("payout.outcome", PayoutOutcomes.Success);   // safe: fixed label
            RondiMetrics.PayoutProcessed(PayoutOutcomes.Success);         // counter, tagged by outcome
            return result;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            // a rule said no = "rejected"; anything else (database, bug) = "error"
            var outcome = ex is DomainException ? PayoutOutcomes.Rejected : PayoutOutcomes.Error;

            activity?.SetTag("payout.outcome", outcome);
            activity?.SetTag("error.type", ex.GetType().Name);            // class name only, never the message
            activity?.SetStatus(ActivityStatusCode.Error);                // a failed payout = error span (no description on purpose)
            RondiMetrics.PayoutProcessed(outcome);
            throw;                                                        // the exception handler still turns it into a response
        }
    }

    // The original 5.x logic, moved here unchanged except for the one cycle-number tag
    private async Task<PayoutResponse> ProcessAsync(
        Guid stokvelId, Guid cycleId, Activity? activity, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        // Retry-on-failure is enabled, so a manual transaction must run inside the execution strategy.
        var strategy = db.Database.CreateExecutionStrategy();

        return await strategy.ExecuteAsync(async () =>
        {
            db.ChangeTracker.Clear(); // clean slate if the strategy retries

            await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync(ct);
            try
            {
                // Stays TRACKED on purpose, because we change its status and save below
                var cycle = await db.ContributionCycles
                    .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.Id == cycleId && c.StokvelId == stokvelId, ct)
                    ?? throw new DomainNotFoundException(
                        $"Cycle {cycleId} was not found for stokvel {stokvelId}.");

                activity?.SetTag("payout.cycle_number", cycle.CycleNumber);   // safe: a sequence number (1, 2, 3...)

                if (cycle.Status != CycleStatus.Open)
                    throw new DomainConflictException("This cycle has already been paid out.");

                // Payouts now record the recipient's UserId (not a StokvelMember.Id)
                var paidUserIds = await db.Payouts
                    .Where(p => p.StokvelId == stokvelId)
                    .Select(p => p.RecipientUserId)
                    .ToListAsync(ct);

                // EDIT 5.2: read-only (untracked) through the dedicated repository, already ordered by rotation
                var next = (await members.GetByStokvelAsync(stokvelId))
                    .FirstOrDefault(m => !paidUserIds.Contains(m.UserId))
                    ?? throw new DomainConflictException("Every member has already been paid in this rotation.");

                var total = await db.Contributions
                    .Where(c => c.CycleId == cycleId)
                    .SumAsync(c => c.Amount, ct);

                if (total <= 0)
                    throw new DomainConflictException("No contributions have been recorded for this cycle.");

                var payout = new Payout(stokvelId, cycleId, next.UserId, total);   // EDIT 5.2: next.UserId
                db.Payouts.Add(payout);
                await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);          // write 1: the payout

                faultHook.AfterPayoutInserted();        // a test can throw here

                cycle.MarkPaidOut();
                await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);          // write 2: the cycle status

                await tx.CommitAsync(ct);

                return new PayoutResponse(
                    payout.Id, payout.StokvelId, payout.CycleId,
                    payout.RecipientUserId, payout.Amount, payout.PaidAtUtc);   // EDIT 5.2
            }
            catch
            {
                await tx.RollbackAsync(ct);
                throw;
            }
        });
    }
}
=== API/Services/StokvelMembershipService.cs ===
using RondiTrack.Data;
using RondiTrack.Domain;

namespace RondiTrack.Services;

public interface IStokvelMembershipService
{
    Task<User> AddMemberAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid userId);
    Task RemoveMemberAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid userId);
}

public sealed class StokvelMembershipService(IStokvelRepository stokvels, IUserRepository users)
    : IStokvelMembershipService
{
    public async Task<User> AddMemberAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid userId)
    {
        var stokvel = await stokvels.GetByIdAsync(stokvelId)
            ?? throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Stokvel {stokvelId} was not found.");

        var user = await users.GetByIdAsync(userId)
            ?? throw new DomainReferenceException($"User {userId} does not exist.");

        stokvel.AddMember(user); // still Stokvel's own rule: no duplicates, no exceeding capacity
        await stokvels.UpdateAsync(stokvel);
        return user;
    }

    public async Task RemoveMemberAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid userId)
    {
        var stokvel = await stokvels.GetByIdAsync(stokvelId)
            ?? throw new DomainNotFoundException($"Stokvel {stokvelId} was not found.");

        if (!stokvel.RemoveMember(userId))
            throw new DomainNotFoundException($"User {userId} is not a member of this stokvel.");

        await stokvels.UpdateAsync(stokvel);
    }
}
=== API/Telemetry/CorrelationId.cs ===
using System.Diagnostics;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http.Features;

namespace RondiTrack.Telemetry;

// The ONE correlation id: the W3C trace id of the request
public static class CorrelationId
{
    public static string For(HttpContext context)
    {
        // the request's own activity (carries the caller's traceparent if they sent one)
        var activity = context.Features.Get<IHttpActivityFeature>()?.Activity ?? Activity.Current;

        return activity is { IdFormat: ActivityIdFormat.W3C }
            ? activity.TraceId.ToString()        // 32 hex characters
            : context.TraceIdentifier;           // fallback if there is no activity (e.g. unit tests)
    }
}
=== API/Telemetry/RondiMetrics.cs ===
using System.Diagnostics.Metrics;

namespace RondiTrack.Telemetry;

// The ONLY allowed tag values. Fixed lists keep every metric small (see README table).
public static class PayoutOutcomes
{
    public const string Success = "success";
    public const string Rejected = "rejected";   // a business rule said no
    public const string Error = "error";         // something broke (database, bug)
}

public static class RefusalResults
{
    public const string NotFound = "not_found";   // non-member got the 404
    public const string Forbidden = "forbidden";  // member (or user) lacked permission
}

// Static on purpose: services stay constructible by hand in unit tests
public static class RondiMetrics
{
    public const string ContributionsRecordedName = "ronditrack.contributions.recorded";
    public const string PayoutsProcessedName = "ronditrack.payouts.processed";
    public const string AuthorizationRefusalsName = "ronditrack.authorization.refusals";

    private static readonly Meter AppMeter = new(RondiTelemetry.MeterName);

    private static readonly Counter<long> Contributions =
        AppMeter.CreateCounter<long>(ContributionsRecordedName, description: "Contributions recorded");   // no tags

    private static readonly Counter<long> Payouts =
        AppMeter.CreateCounter<long>(PayoutsProcessedName, description: "Payouts processed, by outcome");

    private static readonly Counter<long> Refusals =
        AppMeter.CreateCounter<long>(AuthorizationRefusalsName, description: "Authorization refusals, by result");

    public static void ContributionRecorded() => Contributions.Add(1);

    // tag "outcome": success | rejected | error  (3 values)
    public static void PayoutProcessed(string outcome) =>
        Payouts.Add(1, new KeyValuePair<string, object?>("outcome", outcome));

    // tag "result": not_found | forbidden  (2 values)
    public static void AuthorizationRefused(string result) =>
        Refusals.Add(1, new KeyValuePair<string, object?>("result", result));
}
