      Login failed: no matching account
info: RondiTrack.Errors.RondiExceptionHandler[0]
      Request rejected with status 401 (InvalidCredentialsException). CorrelationId: 53b03b591b4fee44a052cf2efc96b4e0

info: RondiTrack.Auth.StokvelAccess[0]

# Assignment 6.3: Ready for the Frontend

> Replace every `<<PASTE>>` with real output from your machine, and every `<<ADAPT>>` with your own name.

## 1. Versioning

- Every route lives under `/api/v1/...`. The prefix is defined once in `ApiRoutes.V1` (`API/Common/ApiRoutes.cs`). Controllers use `[Route($"{ApiRoutes.V1}/...")]`.
- Only one version exists, so there is no versioning library, just a constant.
- **Refresh cookie:** its `Path` moved from `<<old path>>` to `/api/v1/auth`. A browser only sends a cookie to URLs under its `Path`, so leaving the old value would make refresh fail silently. A test proves refresh works at the new path: `Refresh_works_at_versioned_path`.
- **Not versioned:** `/health/live` and `/health/ready`. They are infrastructure probes, not part of the API contract.
- All existing tests had only their URLs updated. No assertion was changed.

## 2. CORS

| Setting | Value |
|---|---|
| Allowed origins | From config `Cors:AllowedOrigins`. `http://localhost:3000` in `appsettings.Development.json`, empty list in `appsettings.json` |
| Methods | `GET, POST, PUT, PATCH, DELETE` (explicit) |
| Request headers | `Content-Type, Authorization, Idempotency-Key, If-Match, If-None-Match` (explicit) |
| Exposed headers | `Retry-After, <<ADAPT: idempotency replay header>>, ETag, Location` |
| Credentials | `AllowCredentials()` |
| Wildcard / origin reflection | Never. Startup throws if an origin contains `*` or a trailing slash |

- The policy is named once (`CorsPolicies.Frontend`). Controllers do not mention CORS.
- **Preflight vs the 6.1 fallback policy:** `UseCors` runs before `UseAuthentication` and `UseAuthorization`.
=== RondiTrack.Tests/AuthenticationTests.cs ===
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using System.Text.Json;
using RondiTrack.Tests.TestSupport;
using Xunit;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

// NEW 6.1: register, login, refresh rotation, reuse detection, logout
[Collection("Postgres collection")]
public class AuthenticationTests(PostgresApiFactory factory)
{
    private const string TestPassword = "a-long-test-password-123";   // throwaway test value, not a real secret
    private const string CookieName = "rondi_refresh";

    private readonly HttpClient _client = factory.CreateAnonymousClient();

    // ---- helpers ----

    private async Task<(string Email, Guid UserId)> RegisterAsync(string? email = null)
    {
        email ??= $"auth{Guid.NewGuid():N}@example.com";
        var response = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/auth/register",
            new { FullName = "Auth Tester", Email = email, Password = TestPassword });
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, response.StatusCode);

        using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync());
        return (email, doc.RootElement.GetProperty("id").GetGuid());
    }

    private Task<HttpResponseMessage> LoginAsync(string email, string password = TestPassword) =>
        _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/auth/login", new { Email = email, Password = password });

    // POST with the refresh cookie set by hand
    private Task<HttpResponseMessage> PostWithCookieAsync(string url, string? cookie)
    {
        var request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Post, url);
        if (cookie is not null) request.Headers.Add("Cookie", $"{CookieName}={cookie}");
        return _client.SendAsync(request);
    }

    private static string? CookieValue(HttpResponseMessage response)
    {
        if (!response.Headers.TryGetValues("Set-Cookie", out var values)) return null;
        var line = values.FirstOrDefault(v => v.StartsWith($"{CookieName}="));
        return line?.Split(';')[0][(CookieName.Length + 1)..];
    }

    private static async Task<string> AccessTokenAsync(HttpResponseMessage response)
    {
        using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync());
        return doc.RootElement.GetProperty("accessToken").GetString()!;
    }

    // ---- registration ----

    [Fact]
    public async Task Register_With_Short_Password_Returns_400_Problem()
    {
        var response = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/auth/register",
            new { FullName = "Short Pass", Email = $"short{Guid.NewGuid():N}@example.com", Password = "tooshort" });

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.BadRequest, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Register_Same_Email_With_Different_Casing_Returns_409()
    {
        var email = $"Lerato{Guid.NewGuid():N}@X.com";
        await RegisterAsync(email);

        var second = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/auth/register",
            new { FullName = "Lerato Again", Email = email.ToLowerInvariant(), Password = TestPassword });

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Conflict, second.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", second.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    // ---- login ----

    [Fact]
    public async Task Login_Returns_Access_Token_In_Body_And_Refresh_Token_Only_In_Cookie()
    {
        var (email, userId) = await RegisterAsync();

        var response = await LoginAsync(email);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, response.StatusCode);

        var cookie = CookieValue(response);
        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrEmpty(cookie));

        // the refresh token must NOT be in the body
        var body = await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync();
        Assert.DoesNotContain(cookie!, body);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("refreshToken", body, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        // cookie flags
        var setCookie = response.Headers.GetValues("Set-Cookie").First(v => v.StartsWith($"{CookieName}=")).ToLowerInvariant();
        Assert.Contains("httponly", setCookie);
        Assert.Contains("secure", setCookie);
        Assert.Contains("samesite=strict", setCookie);
        Assert.Contains("path=/api/auth", setCookie);

        // the access token really works: own account 200, someone else's 403
        using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(body);
        var token = doc.RootElement.GetProperty("accessToken").GetString()!;

        var own = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Get, $"/api/users/{userId}");
        own.Headers.Authorization = new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", token);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, (await _client.SendAsync(own)).StatusCode);

        var other = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Get, $"/api/users/{Guid.NewGuid()}");
        other.Headers.Authorization = new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", token);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Forbidden, (await _client.SendAsync(other)).StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Wrong_Password_And_Unknown_Email_Return_Identical_Problem_Details()
    {
        var (email, _) = await RegisterAsync();

        var wrongPassword = await LoginAsync(email, "totally-wrong-password-1");
        var unknownEmail = await LoginAsync($"nobody{Guid.NewGuid():N}@example.com");

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized, wrongPassword.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized, unknownEmail.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", wrongPassword.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);

        // same status, title and detail (only the correlationId differs)
        Assert.Equal(await ProblemJson.ReadAsync(wrongPassword), await ProblemJson.ReadAsync(unknownEmail));
    }

    // ---- refresh ----

    [Fact]
    public async Task Refresh_Rotates_The_Token()
    {
        var (email, _) = await RegisterAsync();
        var login = await LoginAsync(email);
        var first = CookieValue(login)!;

        var refreshed = await PostWithCookieAsync("/api/auth/refresh", first);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, refreshed.StatusCode);

        var second = CookieValue(refreshed)!;
        Assert.NotEqual(first, second);                         // a NEW refresh token
        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrEmpty(await AccessTokenAsync(refreshed)));

        // the new token works too
        var again = await PostWithCookieAsync("/api/auth/refresh", second);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, again.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Replaying_An_Old_Refresh_Token_Returns_401_And_Kills_The_Newer_Token()
    {
        var (email, _) = await RegisterAsync();
        var oldToken = CookieValue(await LoginAsync(email))!;

        var rotated = await PostWithCookieAsync("/api/auth/refresh", oldToken);
        var newToken = CookieValue(rotated)!;

        // replay the OLD token: 401
        var replay = await PostWithCookieAsync("/api/auth/refresh", oldToken);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized, replay.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", replay.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);

        // the NEWER token is now dead as well (whole family revoked)
        var afterReuse = await PostWithCookieAsync("/api/auth/refresh", newToken);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized, afterReuse.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Refresh_Without_A_Cookie_Returns_401()
    {
        var response = await PostWithCookieAsync("/api/auth/refresh", null);

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    // ---- logout ----

    [Fact]
    public async Task After_Logout_Refreshing_Returns_401()
    {
        var (email, _) = await RegisterAsync();
        var token = CookieValue(await LoginAsync(email))!;

        var logout = await PostWithCookieAsync("/api/auth/logout", token);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NoContent, logout.StatusCode);

        var refresh = await PostWithCookieAsync("/api/auth/refresh", token);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized, refresh.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Logout_Is_Idempotent()
    {
        var (email, _) = await RegisterAsync();
        var token = CookieValue(await LoginAsync(email))!;

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NoContent, (await PostWithCookieAsync("/api/auth/logout", token)).StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NoContent, (await PostWithCookieAsync("/api/auth/logout", token)).StatusCode);     // same token again
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NoContent, (await PostWithCookieAsync("/api/auth/logout", null)).StatusCode);      // no cookie at all
    }

    // ---- secure by default ----

    [Fact]
    public async Task OpenApi_And_Scalar_Load_Without_A_Token()
    {
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, (await _client.GetAsync("/openapi/v1.json")).StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, (await _client.GetAsync("/scalar/v1")).StatusCode);
    }
}
=== RondiTrack.Tests/ConcurrencyAndConstraintTests.cs ===
using RondiTrack.Tests.TestSupport;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Testing;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;
using Npgsql;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Errors;
using Xunit;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

[Collection("Postgres collection")]
public class ConcurrencyAndConstraintTests(PostgresApiFactory factory)
{
    private readonly HttpClient _client = factory.CreateClient();

    // ---------- xmin concurrency ----------

    [Fact]
    public async Task Two_contexts_editing_the_same_payout_raise_DbUpdateConcurrencyException()
    {
        var payoutId = await TestDb.SeedPayoutAsync();

        // TWO contexts are required. Each context remembers the xmin it loaded. With ONE context there is
        // only one copy of the row in memory, and after our own save EF adopts the new xmin, so the context
        // can never see "someone else changed it". Conflicts only exist between two separate views of the row.
        await using var ctxA = TestDb.NewDb();
        await using var ctxB = TestDb.NewDb();

        var a = await ctxA.Payouts.SingleAsync(p => p.Id == payoutId);
        var b = await ctxB.Payouts.SingleAsync(p => p.Id == payoutId);   // both loaded the same version

        ctxA.Entry(a).Property(p => p.Amount).CurrentValue = 200m;
        await ctxA.SaveChangesAsync();                                    // first save wins and moves xmin forward

        ctxB.Entry(b).Property(p => p.Amount).CurrentValue = 300m;
        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<DbUpdateConcurrencyException>(() => ctxB.SaveChangesAsync());   // second is stale
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Http_update_with_a_stale_etag_returns_412_problem()
    {
        // Stokvel is used for the HTTP test because it is one of the entities that has an edit endpoint.
        // Payout has no edit endpoint in RondiTrack, and adding one would be a new feature.
        var created = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/stokvels",
            new { Name = $"Etag {Guid.NewGuid()}", ContributionAmount = 100m, Frequency = 1, MaxMembers = 5 });
        var location = created.Headers.Location!;

        var get = await _client.GetAsync(location);                      // GET: read the ETag
        var etag = get.Headers.ETag!.Tag;

        // First update with the token from the GET: must succeed.
        var first = await PutStokvelAsync(location, "First Name", etag);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, first.StatusCode);

        // Second update with the SAME (now stale) token: must be 412 as a problem response.
        var second = await PutStokvelAsync(location, "Second Name", etag);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.PreconditionFailed, second.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", second.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Http_update_without_if_match_returns_428_problem()
    {
        var created = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/stokvels",
            new { Name = $"NoEtag {Guid.NewGuid()}", ContributionAmount = 100m, Frequency = 1, MaxMembers = 5 });

        var response = await _client.PutAsJsonAsync(created.Headers.Location!,
            new { Name = "x", ContributionAmount = 100m, Frequency = 1, MaxMembers = 5 });

        Assert.Equal((HttpStatusCode)428, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    private Task<HttpResponseMessage> PutStokvelAsync(Uri url, string name, string etag)
    {
        var request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Put, url)
        {
            Content = JsonContent.Create(new { Name = name, ContributionAmount = 100m, Frequency = 1, MaxMembers = 5 })
        };
        request.Headers.TryAddWithoutValidation("If-Match", etag);
        return _client.SendAsync(request);
    }

    // ---------- unique constraints (straight to the database, no service check) ----------

    [Fact]
    public async Task Database_rejects_a_second_contribution_for_the_same_member_and_cycle()
    {
        var (stokvelId, userId, cycleId) = await TestDb.SeedMemberAndCycleAsync();

        await using (var db = TestDb.NewDb())
        {
            db.Contributions.Add(new Contribution(stokvelId, userId, cycleId, 100m));
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        }

        // Straight to the DbContext: ContributionService.ExistsAsync is bypassed, only the constraint is left.
        await using var db2 = TestDb.NewDb();
        db2.Contributions.Add(new Contribution(stokvelId, userId, cycleId, 100m));

        var ex = await Assert.ThrowsAsync<DbUpdateException>(() => db2.SaveChangesAsync());
        Assert.Equal(PostgresErrorCodes.UniqueViolation, Assert.IsType<PostgresException>(ex.InnerException).SqlState);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Database_rejects_paying_the_same_member_twice_in_one_stokvel()
    {
        var (stokvelId, userId, cycleId) = await TestDb.SeedMemberAndCycleAsync();

        await using var db = TestDb.NewDb();
        var cycle2 = new ContributionCycle(stokvelId, 2, 100m);
        db.ContributionCycles.Add(cycle2);
        db.Payouts.Add(new Payout(stokvelId, cycleId, userId, 100m));
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();

        // Different cycle, same recipient, same stokvel: PayoutService would refuse, now the database does too.
        await using var db2 = TestDb.NewDb();
        db2.Payouts.Add(new Payout(stokvelId, cycle2.Id, userId, 100m));

        var ex = await Assert.ThrowsAsync<DbUpdateException>(() => db2.SaveChangesAsync());
        Assert.Equal(PostgresErrorCodes.UniqueViolation, Assert.IsType<PostgresException>(ex.InnerException).SqlState);
    }

    // ---------- the central handler turns 23505 into 409 ----------

    [Fact]
    public async Task Exception_handler_maps_a_unique_violation_to_409()
    {
        var handler = new RondiExceptionHandler(NullLogger<RondiExceptionHandler>.Instance);
        var context = new DefaultHttpContext();
        context.Response.Body = new MemoryStream();

        var postgres = new PostgresException("duplicate key", "ERROR", "ERROR", PostgresErrorCodes.UniqueViolation);
        var handled = await handler.TryHandleAsync(context, new DbUpdateException("dup", postgres), CancellationToken.None);

        Assert.True(handled);
        Assert.Equal(StatusCodes.Status409Conflict, context.Response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", context.Response.ContentType);
    }
}
=== RondiTrack.Tests/ContributionUnitTests.cs ===
using RondiTrack.Data;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Dtos;
using RondiTrack.Services;
using Xunit;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

public class ContributionServiceTests
{
    private static (ContributionService service, IStokvelRepository stokvels, IUserRepository users, IContributionCycleRepository cycles) Build()
    {
        var stokvels = new InMemoryStokvelRepository();
        var users = new InMemoryUserRepository();
        var cycles = new InMemoryContributionCycleRepository();
        var contributions = new InMemoryContributionRepository();
        var idempotency = new InMemoryIdempotencyStore();
        var service = new ContributionService(stokvels, users, cycles, contributions, idempotency);
        return (service, stokvels, users, cycles);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task RecordContribution_Throws_When_Same_User_Pays_Same_Cycle_Twice()
    {
        var built = Build();
        var stokvel = new Stokvel("Test", 100m, ContributionFrequency.Monthly, 5);
        var user = new User("A", "a@example.com");
        stokvel.AddMember(user);
        await built.stokvels.AddAsync(stokvel);
        await built.users.AddAsync(user);
        var cycle = new ContributionCycle(stokvel.Id, 1, 100m);
        await built.cycles.AddAsync(cycle);

        var request = new ContributionRequest(user.Id, cycle.Id, 100m);
        await built.service.RecordContributionAsync(stokvel.Id, request, "key-1");

        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<DomainConflictException>(() =>
            built.service.RecordContributionAsync(stokvel.Id, request, "key-2"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task RecordContribution_Replays_Same_Response_For_Same_Key_And_Same_Body()
    {
        var built = Build();
        var stokvel = new Stokvel("Test", 100m, ContributionFrequency.Monthly, 5);
        var user = new User("A", "a@example.com");
        stokvel.AddMember(user);
        await built.stokvels.AddAsync(stokvel);
        await built.users.AddAsync(user);
        var cycle = new ContributionCycle(stokvel.Id, 1, 100m);
        await built.cycles.AddAsync(cycle);

        var request = new ContributionRequest(user.Id, cycle.Id, 100m);
        var first = await built.service.RecordContributionAsync(stokvel.Id, request, "same-key");
        var second = await built.service.RecordContributionAsync(stokvel.Id, request, "same-key");

        Assert.Equal(first.Response.Id, second.Response.Id);
        Assert.True(second.WasReplayed);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task RecordContribution_Throws_When_Same_Key_Used_With_Different_Body()
    {
        var built = Build();
        var stokvel = new Stokvel("Test", 100m, ContributionFrequency.Monthly, 5);
        var user = new User("A", "a@example.com");
        stokvel.AddMember(user);
        await built.stokvels.AddAsync(stokvel);
        await built.users.AddAsync(user);
        var cycle = new ContributionCycle(stokvel.Id, 1, 100m);
        await built.cycles.AddAsync(cycle);

        var request = new ContributionRequest(user.Id, cycle.Id, 100m);
        await built.service.RecordContributionAsync(stokvel.Id, request, "reused-key");

        var differentRequest = request with { Amount = 200m };

        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<DomainConflictException>(() =>
            built.service.RecordContributionAsync(stokvel.Id, differentRequest, "reused-key"));
    }
}

=== RondiTrack.Tests/ExceptionHandlerLoggingTests.cs ===
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Errors;
using RondiTrack.Tests.TestSupport;
using Xunit;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

// NEW 6.2: the handler's logging rules, tested without HTTP or a database
public class ExceptionHandlerLoggingTests
{
    // builds the real handler with our capturing logger
    private static (RondiExceptionHandler Handler, CapturingLoggerProvider Logs) Build()
    {
        var logs = new CapturingLoggerProvider();
        var loggerFactory = LoggerFactory.Create(b => b.AddProvider(logs));
        return (new RondiExceptionHandler(loggerFactory.CreateLogger<RondiExceptionHandler>()), logs);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task A_4xx_Is_Logged_At_Information_With_The_Type_But_Not_The_Message()
    {
        var (handler, logs) = Build();
        var context = new DefaultHttpContext();
        context.Response.Body = new MemoryStream();   // the handler writes the response here

        await handler.TryHandleAsync(context, new DomainConflictException("secret-person@example.com"), CancellationToken.None);

        var entry = Assert.Single(logs.Entries);
        Assert.Equal(LogLevel.Information, entry.Level);                       // 4xx = Information
        Assert.Contains("DomainConflictException", entry.Message);             // the type is logged
        Assert.DoesNotContain("secret-person@example.com", entry.AllText);    // the message is not
        Assert.Null(entry.Exception);                                          // no stack trace for a 4xx
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task A_5xx_Is_Logged_At_Error_With_The_Exception_And_The_Client_Gets_A_Generic_Detail()
    {
        var (handler, logs) = Build();
        var context = new DefaultHttpContext();
        context.Response.Body = new MemoryStream();

        await handler.TryHandleAsync(context, new InvalidOperationException("internal-detail"), CancellationToken.None);

        var entry = Assert.Single(logs.Entries);
        Assert.Equal(LogLevel.Error, entry.Level);                  // 5xx = Error
        Assert.Contains("internal-detail", entry.Exception);        // the full exception is in the log

        context.Response.Body.Position = 0;
        var body = await new StreamReader(context.Response.Body).ReadToEndAsync();
        Assert.DoesNotContain("internal-detail", body);             // but never in the response
        Assert.Contains("An unexpected error occurred.", body);     // generic detail
        Assert.Contains("correlationId", body);                     // plus the id to quote
    }
}
=== RondiTrack.Tests/HealthAndCorrelationTests.cs ===
using System.Net;
using System.Text.Json;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Tests.TestSupport;
using Xunit;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

// NEW 6.2: probes work without a token, and every problem response carries the caller's trace id
[Collection("Postgres collection")]
public class HealthAndCorrelationTests(PostgresApiFactory factory)
{
    private const string SentTraceId = "0af7651916cd43dd8448eb211c80319c";          // the trace id we send
    private const string Traceparent = $"00-{SentTraceId}-b7ad6b7169203331-01";   // W3C traceparent header value

    // ---- probes ----

    [Fact]
    public async Task Liveness_Returns_200_Without_A_Token()
    {
        var response = await factory.CreateAnonymousClient().GetAsync("/health/live");   // no token on purpose

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("Healthy", await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync());   // only the status word
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Readiness_Returns_200_Without_A_Token()
    {
        var response = await factory.CreateAnonymousClient().GetAsync("/health/ready");

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("Healthy", await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync());
    }

    // ---- correlation id ----

    private static HttpRequestMessage WithTraceparent(string url)
    {
        var request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Get, url);
        request.Headers.Add("traceparent", Traceparent);   // pretend the caller already has a trace
        return request;
    }

    private static async Task<string?> CorrelationIdAsync(HttpResponseMessage response)
    {
        using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync());
        return doc.RootElement.GetProperty("correlationId").GetString();   // read it from the problem json
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Anonymous_401_Returns_The_Sent_Trace_Id_As_CorrelationId()
    {
        var response = await factory.CreateAnonymousClient()
            .SendAsync(WithTraceparent($"/api/stokvels/{Guid.NewGuid()}"));

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal(SentTraceId, await CorrelationIdAsync(response));   // same id we sent
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Non_Member_404_Returns_The_Sent_Trace_Id_As_CorrelationId()
    {
        Guid stokvelId;
        await using (var db = TestDb.NewDb())
        {
            var stokvel = new Stokvel($"Corr {Guid.NewGuid()}", 100m, ContributionFrequency.Monthly, 5);
            db.Stokvels.Add(stokvel);   // a real stokvel with NO members
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            stokvelId = stokvel.Id;
        }

        var outsider = factory.CreateClientFor(Guid.NewGuid());   // signed in, but not a member
        var response = await outsider.SendAsync(WithTraceparent($"/api/stokvels/{stokvelId}"));

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NotFound, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal(SentTraceId, await CorrelationIdAsync(response));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Framework_403_Returns_The_Sent_Trace_Id_As_CorrelationId()
    {
        var user = factory.CreateClientFor(Guid.NewGuid());   // not a platform admin
        var response = await user.SendAsync(WithTraceparent("/api/stokvels"));   // platform admins only

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Forbidden, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal(SentTraceId, await CorrelationIdAsync(response));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Without_A_Traceparent_The_CorrelationId_Is_A_New_32_Character_Trace_Id()
    {
        var response = await factory.CreateAnonymousClient().GetAsync($"/api/stokvels/{Guid.NewGuid()}");

        Assert.Matches("^[0-9a-f]{32}$", await CorrelationIdAsync(response));   // 32 hex characters
    }
}
=== RondiTrack.Tests/IntergrationTest.cs ===
using RondiTrack.Tests.TestSupport;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Testing;
using Xunit;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

[Collection("Postgres collection")]
public class IntegrationTests(PostgresApiFactory factory)
{
    private readonly HttpClient _client = factory.CreateClient();

    [Fact]
    public async Task Create_User_With_Valid_Data_Returns_201()
    {
        var payload = new { FullName = "Zanele Mthembu", Email = $"zanele{Guid.NewGuid()}@example.com" };
        var response = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/users", payload);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, response.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Create_Stokvel_With_Valid_Data_Returns_201()
    {
        var payload = new { Name = $"Test Stokvel {Guid.NewGuid()}", ContributionAmount = 100m, Frequency = 1, MaxMembers = 5 };
        var response = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/stokvels", payload);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, response.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Create_Stokvel_With_MaxMembers_Below_Minimum_Returns_400()
    {
        var payload = new { Name = "Bad Stokvel", ContributionAmount = 100m, Frequency = 1, MaxMembers = 1 };
        var response = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/stokvels", payload);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.BadRequest, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Get_Stokvel_That_Does_Not_Exist_Returns_404()
    {
        var response = await _client.GetAsync($"/api/stokvels/{Guid.NewGuid()}");
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NotFound, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task AddMember_To_Full_Stokvel_Returns_409()
    {
        var stokvel = await CreateStokvelAsync(2);
        var user1 = await CreateUserAsync();
        var user2 = await CreateUserAsync();
        var user3 = await CreateUserAsync();

        await _client.PostAsJsonAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvel.Id}/members", new { UserId = user1.Id });
        await _client.PostAsJsonAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvel.Id}/members", new { UserId = user2.Id });
        var response = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvel.Id}/members", new { UserId = user3.Id });

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Conflict, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task AddMember_With_NonExistent_User_Returns_422()
    {
        var stokvel = await CreateStokvelAsync(5);
        var response = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvel.Id}/members", new { UserId = Guid.NewGuid() });
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.UnprocessableEntity, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Repeated_Contribution_Request_With_Same_Key_Returns_Identical_Response()
    {
        var (stokvelId, userId, cycleId) = await SeedStokvelWithMemberAndCycleAsync();
        var key = $"idem-{Guid.NewGuid()}";
        var payload = new { UserId = userId, CycleId = cycleId, Amount = 100m };

        var response1 = await SendContributionAsync(stokvelId, payload, key);
        var body1 = await response1.Content.ReadAsStringAsync();

        var response2 = await SendContributionAsync(stokvelId, payload, key);
        var body2 = await response2.Content.ReadAsStringAsync();

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, response1.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, response2.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal(body1, body2);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Same_Idempotency_Key_With_Different_Payload_Returns_409()
    {
        var (stokvelId, userId, cycleId) = await SeedStokvelWithMemberAndCycleAsync();
        var key = $"idem-{Guid.NewGuid()}";

        await SendContributionAsync(stokvelId, new { UserId = userId, CycleId = cycleId, Amount = 100m }, key);
        var response2 = await SendContributionAsync(stokvelId, new { UserId = userId, CycleId = cycleId, Amount = 200m }, key);

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Conflict, response2.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response2.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Second_Contribution_For_Same_User_And_Cycle_With_New_Key_Returns_409()
    {
        var (stokvelId, userId, cycleId) = await SeedStokvelWithMemberAndCycleAsync();
        var payload = new { UserId = userId, CycleId = cycleId, Amount = 100m };

        await SendContributionAsync(stokvelId, payload, $"key-{Guid.NewGuid()}");
        var response2 = await SendContributionAsync(stokvelId, payload, $"key-{Guid.NewGuid()}");

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Conflict, response2.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Contribution_Request_Missing_Idempotency_Key_Returns_400()
    {
        var (stokvelId, userId, cycleId) = await SeedStokvelWithMemberAndCycleAsync();
        var payload = new { UserId = userId, CycleId = cycleId, Amount = 100m };
        var response = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvelId}/contributions", payload);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.BadRequest, response.StatusCode);
    }

    // ---- Edge cases ----

    [Fact]
    public async Task GetMembers_For_Stokvel_With_No_Members_Returns_Empty_List()
    {
        var stokvel = await CreateStokvelAsync(5);
        var response = await _client.GetAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvel.Id}/members");

        // EDIT 5.3: the endpoint is paged now, so the body is { items: [], nextPageToken: "" }, not a bare array.
        var page = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<MembersPageDto>();
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Empty(page!.Items);
        Assert.Equal("", page.NextPageToken);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Create_Stokvel_With_MaxMembers_At_Exactly_The_Minimum_Boundary_Succeeds()
    {
        var payload = new { Name = $"Boundary Stokvel {Guid.NewGuid()}", ContributionAmount = 100m, Frequency = 1, MaxMembers = 2 };
        var response = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/stokvels", payload);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, response.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task RecordContribution_For_Cycle_Belonging_To_A_Different_Stokvel_Returns_422()
    {
        var (stokvelAId, userId, _) = await SeedStokvelWithMemberAndCycleAsync();
        var (_, _, cycleBId) = await SeedStokvelWithMemberAndCycleAsync();

        var payload = new { UserId = userId, CycleId = cycleBId, Amount = 100m };
        var response = await SendContributionAsync(stokvelAId, payload, $"key-{Guid.NewGuid()}");

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.UnprocessableEntity, response.StatusCode);
    }

    // ---- Helpers ----

    private async Task<HttpResponseMessage> SendContributionAsync(Guid stokvelId, object payload, string key)
    {
        var request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Post, $"/api/stokvels/{stokvelId}/contributions")
        {
            Content = JsonContent.Create(payload)
        };
        request.Headers.Add("Idempotency-Key", key);
        return await _client.SendAsync(request);
    }

    private async Task<StokvelResponseDto> CreateStokvelAsync(int maxMembers)
    {
        var payload = new { Name = $"Stokvel {Guid.NewGuid()}", ContributionAmount = 100m, Frequency = 1, MaxMembers = maxMembers };
        var response = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/stokvels", payload);
        return (await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<StokvelResponseDto>())!;
    }

    private async Task<UserResponseDto> CreateUserAsync()
    {
        var payload = new { FullName = $"Test User {Guid.NewGuid()}", Email = $"user{Guid.NewGuid()}@example.com" };
        var response = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/users", payload);
        return (await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<UserResponseDto>())!;
    }

    private async Task<(Guid StokvelId, Guid UserId, Guid CycleId)> SeedStokvelWithMemberAndCycleAsync()
    {
        var stokvel = await CreateStokvelAsync(5);
        var user = await CreateUserAsync();
        await _client.PostAsJsonAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvel.Id}/members", new { UserId = user.Id });

        var cyclePayload = new { CycleNumber = 1, TargetAmount = 100m };
        var cycleResponse = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvel.Id}/cycles", cyclePayload);
        var cycle = (await cycleResponse.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<CycleResponseDto>())!;

        return (stokvel.Id, user.Id, cycle.Id);
    }

    private sealed record StokvelResponseDto(Guid Id);
    private sealed record UserResponseDto(Guid Id);
    private sealed record CycleResponseDto(Guid Id);
    private sealed record MembersPageDto(List<object> Items, string NextPageToken);   // ADDED 5.3
}
=== RondiTrack.Tests/LogLeakTests.cs ===
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Tests.TestSupport;
using Xunit;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

// NEW 6.2: the log-leak test. No personal or secret data may reach a log, and a 409 is not an error.
[Collection("Postgres collection")]
public class LogLeakTests(PostgresApiFactory factory)
{
    private const string CookieName = "rondi_refresh";
    private const string Amount = "1234.56";   // a distinctive amount to search for

    private readonly HttpClient _client = factory.CreateAnonymousClient();

    private sealed record IdDto(Guid Id);
    private sealed record TokenDto(string AccessToken);

    // reads the refresh token out of the Set-Cookie header
    private static string? CookieValue(HttpResponseMessage response)
    {
        if (!response.Headers.TryGetValues("Set-Cookie", out var values)) return null;
        var line = values.FirstOrDefault(v => v.StartsWith($"{CookieName}="));
        return line?.Split(';')[0][(CookieName.Length + 1)..];
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task No_Log_Line_Contains_Personal_Or_Secret_Data_And_A_409_Is_Not_An_Error()
    {
        var logStart = factory.CapturedLogs.Entries.Count;   // only look at lines written by THIS test
        var email = $"leak{Guid.NewGuid():N}@example.com";
        var password = $"pw-{Guid.NewGuid():N}-xyz";

        // 1. register
        var register = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/auth/register",
            new { FullName = "Leak Tester", Email = email, Password = password });
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, register.StatusCode);
        var userId = (await register.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<IdDto>())!.Id;

        // 2. log in
        var login = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/auth/login", new { Email = email, Password = password });
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, login.StatusCode);
        var accessToken1 = (await login.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<TokenDto>())!.AccessToken;
        var refreshToken1 = CookieValue(login)!;

        // 3. refresh (rotates both tokens)
        var refreshRequest = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Post, "/api/auth/refresh");
        refreshRequest.Headers.Add("Cookie", $"{CookieName}={refreshToken1}");
        var refresh = await _client.SendAsync(refreshRequest);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, refresh.StatusCode);
        var accessToken2 = (await refresh.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<TokenDto>())!.AccessToken;
        var refreshToken2 = CookieValue(refresh)!;

        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrEmpty(accessToken1));   // so we never search for an empty string
        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrEmpty(refreshToken2));

        // 4. record a contribution with the distinctive amount (user joins a fresh stokvel)
        Guid stokvelId, cycleId;
        await using (var db = TestDb.NewDb())
        {
            var stokvel = new Stokvel($"Leak {Guid.NewGuid()}", 100m, ContributionFrequency.Monthly, 5);
            db.Stokvels.Add(stokvel);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();   // parent first

            var cycle = new ContributionCycle(stokvel.Id, 1, 100m);
            db.StokvelMembers.Add(new StokvelMember(stokvel.Id, userId, 1));
            db.ContributionCycles.Add(cycle);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            stokvelId = stokvel.Id;
            cycleId = cycle.Id;
        }

        var contribution = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Post, $"/api/stokvels/{stokvelId}/contributions")
        {
            Content = JsonContent.Create(new { UserId = userId, CycleId = cycleId, Amount = 1234.56m })
        };
        contribution.Headers.Authorization = new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", accessToken2);
        contribution.Headers.Add("Idempotency-Key", $"leak-{Guid.NewGuid()}");
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, (await _client.SendAsync(contribution)).StatusCode);

        // 5. trigger the duplicate-registration 409
        var beforeConflict = factory.CapturedLogs.Entries.Count;   // remember where the 409 logs start
        var duplicate = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/auth/register",
            new { FullName = "Leak Again", Email = email, Password = password });
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Conflict, duplicate.StatusCode);

        var all = factory.CapturedLogs.Entries.Skip(logStart).ToList();   // all lines from this test

        // the 409 must not be an Error, and the handler must have logged it at Information
        var forConflict = factory.CapturedLogs.Entries.Skip(beforeConflict).ToList();
        Assert.DoesNotContain(forConflict, e => e.Level >= LogLevel.Error);
        Assert.Contains(forConflict, e => e.Level == LogLevel.Information && e.Message.Contains("409"));

        // nothing secret or personal anywhere in the captured logs
        var secrets = new[] { email, password, accessToken1, accessToken2, refreshToken1, refreshToken2, Amount, "1234,56" };
        foreach (var entry in all)
            foreach (var secret in secrets)
                Assert.DoesNotContain(secret, entry.AllText, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    }
}
=== RondiTrack.Tests/PagingTests.cs ===
using RondiTrack.Tests.TestSupport;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Testing;
using Xunit;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

// NEW 5.3: proves the paging contract through the real HTTP endpoint.
[Collection("Postgres collection")]
public class PagingTests(PostgresApiFactory factory)
{
    private readonly HttpClient _client = factory.CreateClient();

    // Builds a stokvel with 3 members who each contributed once. Returns the list URL.
    private async Task<string> SeedThreeContributionsAsync()
    {
        var stokvel = (await (await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/stokvels",
            new { Name = $"Paging {Guid.NewGuid()}", ContributionAmount = 100m, Frequency = 1, MaxMembers = 5 }))
            .Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<IdDto>())!;

        var cycle = (await (await _client.PostAsJsonAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvel.Id}/cycles",
            new { CycleNumber = 1, TargetAmount = 100m })).Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<IdDto>())!;

        for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++)
        {
            var user = (await (await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/users",
                new { FullName = $"Pager {i}", Email = $"pager{Guid.NewGuid()}@example.com" }))
                .Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<IdDto>())!;
            await _client.PostAsJsonAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvel.Id}/members", new { UserId = user.Id });

            var request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Post, $"/api/stokvels/{stokvel.Id}/contributions")
            {
                Content = JsonContent.Create(new { UserId = user.Id, CycleId = cycle.Id, Amount = 100m })
            };
            request.Headers.Add("Idempotency-Key", $"k-{Guid.NewGuid()}");
            await _client.SendAsync(request);
        }

        return $"/api/stokvels/{stokvel.Id}/cycles/{cycle.Id}/contributions";
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Walking_all_pages_returns_every_row_once_and_ends_with_an_empty_token()
    {
        var url = await SeedThreeContributionsAsync();
        var seen = new List<Guid>();
        var token = "";

        do
        {
            var query = $"{url}?pageSize=2" + (token == "" ? "" : $"&pageToken={Uri.EscapeDataString(token)}");
            var page = (await _client.GetFromJsonAsync<PageDto>(query))!;
            seen.AddRange(page.Items.Select(i => i.Id));
            token = page.NextPageToken;
        } while (token != "");

        Assert.Equal(3, seen.Count);
        Assert.Equal(3, seen.Distinct().Count());
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Negative_page_size_returns_400()
    {
        var url = await SeedThreeContributionsAsync();
        var response = await _client.GetAsync($"{url}?pageSize=-1");

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.BadRequest, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Unknown_sort_field_returns_400()
    {
        var url = await SeedThreeContributionsAsync();
        var response = await _client.GetAsync($"{url}?sort=memberName");

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.BadRequest, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Token_reused_with_a_different_sort_returns_400()
    {
        var url = await SeedThreeContributionsAsync();
        var first = (await _client.GetFromJsonAsync<PageDto>($"{url}?pageSize=1"))!;
        Assert.NotEqual("", first.NextPageToken);

        var response = await _client.GetAsync(
            $"{url}?pageSize=1&sort=-date&pageToken={Uri.EscapeDataString(first.NextPageToken)}");

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.BadRequest, response.StatusCode);
    }

    private sealed record IdDto(Guid Id);
    private sealed record PageDto(List<IdDto> Items, string NextPageToken);
}
=== RondiTrack.Tests/PayoutTransactionTest.cs ===
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using RondiTrack.Data;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Services;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

[Collection("Postgres collection")]   // CHANGED 5.4: makes sure the Testcontainers database is started first
public class PayoutTransactionTests
{
    // A hook that throws between the two writes, to force a failure mid-transaction
    private sealed class ThrowingHook : IPayoutFaultHook
    {
        public void AfterPayoutInserted() =>
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Simulated failure after the payout insert");
    }

    // CHANGED 5.4: no more user-secrets, so it uses the Testcontainers database through TestDb
    private static RondiTrackDbContext NewDb() => TestDb.NewDb();

    // EDIT 5.2: with real foreign keys the parents (users, stokvel) must be saved BEFORE members, cycle and contributions.
    // It now returns the first member's UserId (StokvelMember has no Id any more).
    private static async Task<(Guid StokvelId, Guid CycleId, Guid FirstUserId)> SeedAsync()
    {
        using var db = NewDb();

        var stokvel = new Stokvel($"Payout Test {Guid.NewGuid()}", 100m, ContributionFrequency.Monthly, 5);
        var user1 = new User("Payout One", $"p1-{Guid.NewGuid()}@example.com");
        var user2 = new User("Payout Two", $"p2-{Guid.NewGuid()}@example.com");
        var cycle = new ContributionCycle(stokvel.Id, 1, 200m);

        db.Users.AddRange(user1, user2);
        db.Stokvels.Add(stokvel);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();                       // parents first

        db.StokvelMembers.AddRange(
            new StokvelMember(stokvel.Id, user1.Id, 1),
            new StokvelMember(stokvel.Id, user2.Id, 2));
        db.ContributionCycles.Add(cycle);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();                       // then members and cycle

        db.Contributions.AddRange(
            new Contribution(stokvel.Id, user1.Id, cycle.Id, 100m),
            new Contribution(stokvel.Id, user2.Id, cycle.Id, 100m));
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();                       // then contributions

        return (stokvel.Id, cycle.Id, user1.Id);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ProcessNextPayout_WhenItFailsAfterThePayoutInsert_LeavesNothingBehind()
    {
        var (stokvelId, cycleId, _) = await SeedAsync();

        using (var db = NewDb())
        {
            // EDIT 5.2: PayoutService now takes the member repository too
            var service = new PayoutService(db, new ThrowingHook(), new EfStokvelMemberRepository(db));
            await Assert.ThrowsAsync<InvalidOperationException>(
                () => service.ProcessNextPayoutAsync(stokvelId, cycleId));
        }

        // Re-query with a brand new DbContext: the database is the evidence, not a status code
        using var verify = NewDb();
        Assert.Equal(0, await verify.Payouts.CountAsync(p => p.CycleId == cycleId));

        var cycle = await verify.ContributionCycles.SingleAsync(c => c.Id == cycleId);
        Assert.Equal(CycleStatus.Open, cycle.Status);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ProcessNextPayout_PaysTheFirstMemberInRotationAndMarksTheCyclePaidOut()
    {
        var (stokvelId, cycleId, firstUserId) = await SeedAsync();

        using (var db = NewDb())
        {
            var result = await new PayoutService(db, new NoOpPayoutFaultHook(), new EfStokvelMemberRepository(db))
                .ProcessNextPayoutAsync(stokvelId, cycleId);

            Assert.Equal(firstUserId, result.RecipientUserId);   // EDIT 5.2: was RecipientMemberId
            Assert.Equal(200m, result.Amount);
        }

        using var verify = NewDb();
        Assert.Equal(1, await verify.Payouts.CountAsync(p => p.CycleId == cycleId));

        var cycle = await verify.ContributionCycles.SingleAsync(c => c.Id == cycleId);
        Assert.Equal(CycleStatus.PaidOut, cycle.Status);
    }
}

=== RondiTrack.Tests/StokvelAuthorizationTests.cs ===
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Tests.TestSupport;
using Xunit;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

// NEW 6.1: the authorization matrix, checked against the real database
[Collection("Postgres collection")]
public class StokvelAuthorizationTests(PostgresApiFactory factory)
{
    // ---- helpers ----

    private static async Task<Guid> CreateUserAsync()
    {
        await using var db = TestDb.NewDb();
        var user = new User("Test User", $"u-{Guid.NewGuid()}@example.com");
        db.Users.Add(user);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        return user.Id;
    }

    // a stokvel + one cycle + memberships with the roles you ask for (users must already exist)
    private static async Task<(Guid StokvelId, Guid CycleId)> SeedStokvelAsync(
        params (Guid UserId, StokvelRole Role)[] members)
    {
        await using var db = TestDb.NewDb();

        var stokvel = new Stokvel($"Auth {Guid.NewGuid()}", 100m, ContributionFrequency.Monthly, 5);
        db.Stokvels.Add(stokvel);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();                       // parent first

        var cycle = new ContributionCycle(stokvel.Id, 1, 100m);
        var position = 1;
        foreach (var (userId, role) in members)
            db.StokvelMembers.Add(new StokvelMember(stokvel.Id, userId, position++, role));
        db.ContributionCycles.Add(cycle);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();

        return (stokvel.Id, cycle.Id);
    }

    private static async Task AddContributionAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid userId, Guid cycleId)
    {
        await using var db = TestDb.NewDb();
        db.Contributions.Add(new Contribution(stokvelId, userId, cycleId, 100m));
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
    }

    private static async Task RemoveMembershipAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid userId)
    {
        await using var db = TestDb.NewDb();
        await db.StokvelMembers.Where(m => m.StokvelId == stokvelId && m.UserId == userId).ExecuteDeleteAsync();
    }

    private static Task<HttpResponseMessage> RecordAsync(HttpClient client, Guid stokvelId, Guid userId, Guid cycleId)
    {
        var request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Post, $"/api/stokvels/{stokvelId}/contributions")
        {
            Content = JsonContent.Create(new { UserId = userId, CycleId = cycleId, Amount = 100m })
        };
        request.Headers.Add("Idempotency-Key", $"k-{Guid.NewGuid()}");
        return client.SendAsync(request);
    }

    // the endpoint used for the 404 / 403 / 201 comparison: open a cycle (officer only)
    private static Task<HttpResponseMessage> OpenCycleAsync(HttpClient client, Guid stokvelId) =>
        client.PostAsJsonAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvelId}/cycles", new { CycleNumber = 2, TargetAmount = 100m });

    // ---- 401 ----

    [Fact]
    public async Task Request_With_No_Token_Returns_401_Problem()
    {
        var response = await factory.CreateAnonymousClient().GetAsync($"/api/stokvels/{Guid.NewGuid()}");

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Request_With_A_Garbage_Token_Returns_401_Problem()
    {
        var client = factory.CreateAnonymousClient();
        client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization = new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", "not.a.real-token");

        var response = await client.GetAsync($"/api/stokvels/{Guid.NewGuid()}");

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    // ---- 404 / 403 / success on the SAME endpoint ----

    [Fact]
    public async Task Non_Member_Gets_404_On_An_Officer_Endpoint()
    {
        var outsider = await CreateUserAsync();
        var (stokvelId, _) = await SeedStokvelAsync();

        var response = await OpenCycleAsync(factory.CreateClientFor(outsider), stokvelId);

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NotFound, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Member_Without_The_Role_Gets_403_On_The_Same_Endpoint()
    {
        var member = await CreateUserAsync();
        var (stokvelId, _) = await SeedStokvelAsync((member, StokvelRole.Member));

        var response = await OpenCycleAsync(factory.CreateClientFor(member), stokvelId);

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Forbidden, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(StokvelRole.Treasurer)]
    [InlineData(StokvelRole.Admin)]   // Admin is the chairperson role from 5.2
    public async Task Treasurer_Or_Chairperson_Succeeds_On_The_Same_Endpoint(StokvelRole role)
    {
        var officer = await CreateUserAsync();
        var (stokvelId, _) = await SeedStokvelAsync((officer, role));

        var response = await OpenCycleAsync(factory.CreateClientFor(officer), stokvelId);

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, response.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Platform_Admin_Succeeds_On_The_Same_Endpoint()
    {
        var (stokvelId, _) = await SeedStokvelAsync();

        var response = await OpenCycleAsync(factory.CreateClient(), stokvelId);   // default client = platform admin

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, response.StatusCode);
    }

    // ---- refusal must not leak ----

    [Fact]
    public async Task Non_Member_404_Looks_The_Same_As_A_Stokvel_That_Does_Not_Exist()
    {
        var outsider = await CreateUserAsync();
        var (realId, _) = await SeedStokvelAsync();
        var fakeId = Guid.NewGuid();
        var client = factory.CreateClientFor(outsider);

        var real = await client.GetAsync($"/api/stokvels/{realId}");
        var fake = await client.GetAsync($"/api/stokvels/{fakeId}");

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NotFound, real.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NotFound, fake.StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", real.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);
        Assert.Equal("application/problem+json", fake.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType);

        // same status, title and detail once the id is blanked out
        var (realStatus, realTitle, realDetail) = await ProblemJson.ReadAsync(real);
        var (fakeStatus, fakeTitle, fakeDetail) = await ProblemJson.ReadAsync(fake);
        Assert.Equal(realStatus, fakeStatus);
        Assert.Equal(realTitle, fakeTitle);
        Assert.Equal(realDetail!.Replace(realId.ToString(), "{id}"), fakeDetail!.Replace(fakeId.ToString(), "{id}"));
    }

    // ---- what a member may do ----

    [Fact]
    public async Task Member_Can_View_Stokvel_Cycles_And_Contributions()
    {
        var member = await CreateUserAsync();
        var (stokvelId, cycleId) = await SeedStokvelAsync((member, StokvelRole.Member));
        var client = factory.CreateClientFor(member);

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, (await client.GetAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvelId}")).StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, (await client.GetAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvelId}/cycles")).StatusCode);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, (await client.GetAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvelId}/cycles/{cycleId}/contributions")).StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Member_Records_Their_Own_Contribution()
    {
        var member = await CreateUserAsync();
        var (stokvelId, cycleId) = await SeedStokvelAsync((member, StokvelRole.Member));

        var response = await RecordAsync(factory.CreateClientFor(member), stokvelId, member, cycleId);

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, response.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Member_Is_Refused_When_Recording_Someone_Elses_Contribution()
    {
        var member = await CreateUserAsync();
        var other = await CreateUserAsync();
        var (stokvelId, cycleId) = await SeedStokvelAsync((member, StokvelRole.Member), (other, StokvelRole.Member));

        var response = await RecordAsync(factory.CreateClientFor(member), stokvelId, other, cycleId);

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Forbidden, response.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Treasurer_Can_Record_A_Contribution_For_Any_Member()
    {
        var treasurer = await CreateUserAsync();
        var member = await CreateUserAsync();
        var (stokvelId, cycleId) = await SeedStokvelAsync((treasurer, StokvelRole.Treasurer), (member, StokvelRole.Member));

        var response = await RecordAsync(factory.CreateClientFor(treasurer), stokvelId, member, cycleId);

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, response.StatusCode);
    }

    // ---- payouts ----

    [Fact]
    public async Task Member_Cannot_Process_A_Payout_But_A_Treasurer_Can()
    {
        var treasurer = await CreateUserAsync();
        var member = await CreateUserAsync();
        var (stokvelId, cycleId) = await SeedStokvelAsync((treasurer, StokvelRole.Treasurer), (member, StokvelRole.Member));
        await AddContributionAsync(stokvelId, member, cycleId);   // a payout needs at least one contribution

        var url = $"/api/stokvels/{stokvelId}/cycles/{cycleId}/payouts";

        var asMember = await factory.CreateClientFor(member).PostAsync(url, null);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Forbidden, asMember.StatusCode);

        var asTreasurer = await factory.CreateClientFor(treasurer).PostAsync(url, null);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, asTreasurer.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Non_Member_Gets_404_On_The_Payout_Endpoint()
    {
        var outsider = await CreateUserAsync();
        var member = await CreateUserAsync();
        var (stokvelId, cycleId) = await SeedStokvelAsync((member, StokvelRole.Treasurer));

        var response = await factory.CreateClientFor(outsider)
            .PostAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvelId}/cycles/{cycleId}/payouts", null);

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NotFound, response.StatusCode);
    }

    // ---- the core design point ----

    [Fact]
    public async Task Removed_Member_With_A_Still_Valid_Token_Is_Refused_At_Once()
    {
        var treasurer = await CreateUserAsync();
        var (stokvelId, _) = await SeedStokvelAsync((treasurer, StokvelRole.Treasurer));
        var client = factory.CreateClientFor(treasurer);   // ONE token, used before and after

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, (await client.GetAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvelId}")).StatusCode);

        await RemoveMembershipAsync(stokvelId, treasurer);   // the chairperson removes them

        // same token, still not expired, but the database says they are out
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NotFound, (await client.GetAsync($"/api/stokvels/{stokvelId}")).StatusCode);
    }
}
=== RondiTrack.Tests/StokvelUnitTests.cs ===
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using Xunit;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

public class StokvelMembershipTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void AddMember_Throws_When_Stokvel_Is_Full()
    {
        var stokvel = new Stokvel("Test", 100m, ContributionFrequency.Monthly, 2);
        var user1 = new User("A", "a@example.com");
        var user2 = new User("B", "b@example.com");
        var user3 = new User("C", "c@example.com");
        stokvel.AddMember(user1);
        stokvel.AddMember(user2);

        Assert.Throws<DomainConflictException>(() => stokvel.AddMember(user3));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddMember_Throws_When_User_Already_A_Member()
    {
        var stokvel = new Stokvel("Test", 100m, ContributionFrequency.Monthly, 5);
        var user = new User("A", "a@example.com");
        stokvel.AddMember(user);

        Assert.Throws<DomainConflictException>(() => stokvel.AddMember(user));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddMember_Succeeds_When_Space_Available_And_Not_Already_Member()
    {
        var stokvel = new Stokvel("Test", 100m, ContributionFrequency.Monthly, 5);
        var user = new User("A", "a@example.com");

        stokvel.AddMember(user);

        Assert.True(stokvel.HasMember(user.Id));
    }
}
=== RondiTrack.Tests/TelemetryTests.cs ===
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Diagnostics.Metrics;
using RondiTrack.Data;
using RondiTrack.Domain;
using RondiTrack.Dtos;
using RondiTrack.Services;
using RondiTrack.Telemetry;
using Xunit;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

// NEW 6.2: the payout metric and the payout.process span
[Collection("Postgres collection")]   // starts the Testcontainers database first (TestDb needs it)
public class TelemetryTests
{
    // throws between the two writes, to force a failed payout
    private sealed class ThrowingHook : IPayoutFaultHook
    {
        public void AfterPayoutInserted() => throw new InvalidOperationException("Simulated failure");
    }

    // a stokvel with one member, one cycle and one contribution, so a payout can succeed
    private static async Task<(Guid StokvelId, Guid CycleId)> SeedPayableCycleAsync()
    {
        var (stokvelId, userId, cycleId) = await TestDb.SeedMemberAndCycleAsync();
        await using var db = TestDb.NewDb();
        db.Contributions.Add(new Contribution(stokvelId, userId, cycleId, 100m));
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        return (stokvelId, cycleId);
    }

    // builds the real PayoutService and runs one payout
    private static Task<PayoutResponse> PayAsync(Guid stokvelId, Guid cycleId, IPayoutFaultHook? hook = null)
    {
        var db = TestDb.NewDb();
        return new PayoutService(db, hook ?? new NoOpPayoutFaultHook(), new EfStokvelMemberRepository(db))
            .ProcessNextPayoutAsync(stokvelId, cycleId);
    }

    // built-in MeterListener: records the "outcome" tag of every payouts measurement
    private static MeterListener ListenToPayouts(List<string?> outcomes)
    {
        var listener = new MeterListener();
        listener.InstrumentPublished = (instrument, l) =>
        {
            if (instrument.Meter.Name == RondiTelemetry.MeterName && instrument.Name == RondiMetrics.PayoutsProcessedName)
                l.EnableMeasurementEvents(instrument);   // only listen to our payouts counter
        };
        listener.SetMeasurementEventCallback<long>((instrument, value, tags, state) =>
        {
            string? outcome = null;
            foreach (var tag in tags)
                if (tag.Key == "outcome") outcome = tag.Value?.ToString();
            lock (outcomes) outcomes.Add(outcome);
        });
        listener.Start();
        return listener;
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task A_Successful_Payout_Increments_The_Payouts_Counter_With_Outcome_Success()
    {
        var (stokvelId, cycleId) = await SeedPayableCycleAsync();
        var outcomes = new List<string?>();
        using var listener = ListenToPayouts(outcomes);

        await PayAsync(stokvelId, cycleId);

        lock (outcomes) Assert.Contains("success", outcomes);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task A_Refused_Payout_Is_Counted_As_Rejected_And_A_Broken_One_As_Error()
    {
        var (stokvelId, cycleId) = await SeedPayableCycleAsync();
        var outcomes = new List<string?>();
        using var listener = ListenToPayouts(outcomes);

        await PayAsync(stokvelId, cycleId);                                                       // first payout works
        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<DomainConflictException>(() => PayAsync(stokvelId, cycleId));   // already paid = rejected

        var (stokvel2, cycle2) = await SeedPayableCycleAsync();
        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<InvalidOperationException>(
            () => PayAsync(stokvel2, cycle2, new ThrowingHook()));                                // a bug mid-transaction = error

        lock (outcomes)
        {
            Assert.Contains("rejected", outcomes);
            Assert.Contains("error", outcomes);
        }
    }

    // records finished payout.process spans
    private static ActivityListener ListenToSpans(List<Activity> spans)
    {
        var listener = new ActivityListener
        {
            ShouldListenTo = source => source.Name == RondiTelemetry.ActivitySourceName,   // only our source
            Sample = (ref ActivityCreationOptions<ActivityContext> _) => ActivitySamplingResult.AllDataAndRecorded,
            ActivityStopped = activity => { lock (spans) spans.Add(activity); }
        };
        ActivitySource.AddActivityListener(listener);
        return listener;
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Payout_Span_Carries_Safe_Tags_And_No_Error_Status_On_Success()
    {
        var (stokvelId, cycleId) = await SeedPayableCycleAsync();
        var spans = new List<Activity>();
        using var listener = ListenToSpans(spans);

        await PayAsync(stokvelId, cycleId);

        Activity span;
        lock (spans) span = Assert.Single(spans, a => a.OperationName == "payout.process");
        Assert.Equal("success", span.GetTagItem("payout.outcome")?.ToString());
        Assert.Equal("1", span.GetTagItem("payout.cycle_number")?.ToString());
        Assert.NotEqual(ActivityStatusCode.Error, span.Status);

        // the payout was 100: no tag value may contain the amount
        Assert.All(span.TagObjects, t => Assert.DoesNotContain("100", t.Value?.ToString() ?? ""));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task A_Failed_Payout_Sets_The_Span_Status_To_Error()
    {
        var (stokvelId, cycleId) = await SeedPayableCycleAsync();
        var spans = new List<Activity>();
        using var listener = ListenToSpans(spans);

        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<InvalidOperationException>(() => PayAsync(stokvelId, cycleId, new ThrowingHook()));

        Activity span;
        lock (spans) span = Assert.Single(spans, a => a.OperationName == "payout.process");
        Assert.Equal(ActivityStatusCode.Error, span.Status);
        Assert.Equal("error", span.GetTagItem("payout.outcome")?.ToString());
        Assert.Equal("InvalidOperationException", span.GetTagItem("error.type")?.ToString());
    }
}
=== RondiTrack.Tests/Test.Db.cs ===
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using RondiTrack.Data;
using RondiTrack.Domain;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

// NEW 5.3: helpers so tests can talk to PostgreSQL directly with their own DbContexts.
public static class TestDb
{
    // CHANGED 5.4: the factory sets this to the Testcontainers connection string
    public static string? ConnectionString { get; set; }

    // Every call returns a brand new, independent DbContext (own connection, own tracking).
    public static RondiTrackDbContext NewDb()
    {
        // CHANGED 5.4: no more user-secrets, so tests can never touch the dev database
        var connectionString = ConnectionString
            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Test container is not started. Add [Collection(\"Postgres collection\")] to the test class.");

        var options = new DbContextOptionsBuilder<RondiTrackDbContext>()
            .UseNpgsql(connectionString, n => n.EnableRetryOnFailure(4, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10), null))
            .Options;

        return new RondiTrackDbContext(options);
    }

    // A brand new stokvel + user + member + cycle, so a test never depends on leftover data.
    public static async Task<(Guid StokvelId, Guid UserId, Guid CycleId)> SeedMemberAndCycleAsync()
    {
        await using var db = NewDb();

        var stokvel = new Stokvel($"Test {Guid.NewGuid()}", 100m, ContributionFrequency.Monthly, 5);
        var user = new User("Test Member", $"t-{Guid.NewGuid()}@example.com");
        db.Users.Add(user);
        db.Stokvels.Add(stokvel);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();                 // parents first

        var cycle = new ContributionCycle(stokvel.Id, 1, 100m);
        db.StokvelMembers.Add(new StokvelMember(stokvel.Id, user.Id, 1));
        db.ContributionCycles.Add(cycle);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();

        return (stokvel.Id, user.Id, cycle.Id);
    }

    public static async Task<Guid> SeedPayoutAsync()
    {
        var (stokvelId, userId, cycleId) = await SeedMemberAndCycleAsync();
        await using var db = NewDb();
        var payout = new Payout(stokvelId, cycleId, userId, 100m);
        db.Payouts.Add(payout);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        return payout.Id;
    }
}
=== RondiTrack.Tests/TestSupport/CapturingLoggerProvider.cs ===
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests.TestSupport;

// One captured log line: the text, the exception text and the structured values
public sealed record CapturedLog(string Category, LogLevel Level, string Message, string? Exception, string Properties)
{
    public string AllText => $"{Message} {Exception} {Properties}";   // everything we search for leaks
}

// Test-only logger provider that remembers every log line in memory
public sealed class CapturingLoggerProvider : ILoggerProvider
{
    private readonly ConcurrentQueue<CapturedLog> _entries = new();   // safe when many requests log at once

    public IReadOnlyList<CapturedLog> Entries => _entries.ToArray();   // a snapshot copy

    public ILogger CreateLogger(string categoryName) => new CapturingLogger(categoryName, _entries);

    public void Dispose() { }

    private sealed class CapturingLogger(string category, ConcurrentQueue<CapturedLog> sink) : ILogger
    {
        public IDisposable? BeginScope<TState>(TState state) where TState : notnull => null;

        public bool IsEnabled(LogLevel logLevel) => true;   // capture every level

        public void Log<TState>(LogLevel logLevel, EventId eventId, TState state, Exception? exception,
            Func<TState, Exception?, string> formatter)
        {
            var properties = state is IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, object?>> values
                ? string.Join(";", values.Select(v => $"{v.Key}={v.Value}"))   // structured values too
                : "";
            sink.Enqueue(new CapturedLog(category, logLevel, formatter(state, exception), exception?.ToString(), properties));
        }
    }
}
=== RondiTrack.Tests/TestSupport/PostgresApiFactory.cs ===
namespace RondiTrack.Tests.TestSupport;                       // folder is TestSupport, NOT Infrastructure

using System.Net.Http.Headers;                                // AuthenticationHeaderValue
using System.Security.Cryptography;                           // random test signing key
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Hosting;                           // IWebHostBuilder
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Testing;                       // WebApplicationFactory
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;                          // UseNpgsql, MigrateAsync
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;               // GetRequiredService
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;                           // NEW 6.2: needed for AddProvider
using RondiTrack.Auth;                                        // ITokenService
using RondiTrack.Data;                                        // RondiTrackDbContext
using Testcontainers.PostgreSql;                              // the PostgreSQL container

public class PostgresApiFactory : WebApplicationFactory<Program>, IAsyncLifetime
{
    private readonly PostgreSqlContainer _container = new PostgreSqlBuilder("postgres:17")   // CHANGED: image goes in the constructor (removes the obsolete warning)
        .WithDatabase("ronditrack_test")                      // test database name
        .WithUsername("postgres")                             // test username
        .WithPassword("postgres")                             // test password
        .Build();                                             // create the container object

    // NEW 6.1: a random signing key made fresh on every run. Nothing is committed.
    private readonly string _jwtKey = Convert.ToBase64String(RandomNumberGenerator.GetBytes(48));

    // NEW 6.2: every log line the app writes during a test run ends up here
    public CapturingLoggerProvider CapturedLogs { get; } = new();

    public async Task InitializeAsync()
    {
        await _container.StartAsync();                        // start the Docker container first

        TestDb.ConnectionString = _container.GetConnectionString();   // TestDb helpers use the container too

        // Program.cs seeds data at startup (Development), so the tables must exist BEFORE the app starts.
        var options = new DbContextOptionsBuilder<RondiTrackDbContext>()
            .UseNpgsql(_container.GetConnectionString())      // point at the container
            .Options;                                         // build the options
        await using var db = new RondiTrackDbContext(options); // temporary context just for migrating
        await db.Database.MigrateAsync();                     // create all tables in the empty container
    }

    protected override void ConfigureWebHost(IWebHostBuilder builder)
    {
        builder.ConfigureLogging(logging => logging.AddProvider(CapturedLogs));   // NEW 6.2: capture logs for the leak test

        // UseSetting (NOT ConfigureAppConfiguration) so Program.cs sees it at startup.
        // "RondiTrack" is the exact key your Program.cs reads.
        builder.UseSetting("ConnectionStrings:RondiTrack", _container.GetConnectionString());
        builder.UseSetting("Jwt:SigningKey", _jwtKey);        // NEW 6.1: the app refuses to start without it
    }

    // NEW 6.1: every client made with CreateClient() starts signed in as a platform admin.
    // That is how the old tests (which know nothing about auth) keep passing without any change.
    protected override void ConfigureClient(HttpClient client)
    {
        base.ConfigureClient(client);
        client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization =
            new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", MintToken(Guid.NewGuid(), platformAdmin: true));
    }

    // NEW 6.1: makes a real access token with the app's own TokenService (same key, same validation path)
    public string MintToken(Guid userId, bool platformAdmin = false) =>
        Services.GetRequiredService<ITokenService>().CreateAccessToken(userId, platformAdmin).Value;

    // NEW 6.1: a client signed in as one specific (non-admin) user
    public HttpClient CreateClientFor(Guid userId)
    {
        var client = CreateClient();
        client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization = new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", MintToken(userId));
        return client;
    }

    // NEW 6.1: a client with no token. Uses https and no cookie handling, so tests read Set-Cookie by hand.
    public HttpClient CreateAnonymousClient()
    {
        var client = CreateClient(new WebApplicationFactoryClientOptions
        {
            BaseAddress = new Uri("https://localhost"),
            HandleCookies = false,
            AllowAutoRedirect = false
        });
        client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization = null;
        return client;
    }

    async Task IAsyncLifetime.DisposeAsync() => await _container.DisposeAsync(); // remove container after tests
}

[CollectionDefinition("Postgres collection")]                 // name the test classes use
public class PostgresCollection : ICollectionFixture<PostgresApiFactory> { } // one shared container for all tests
=== RondiTrack.Tests/TestSupport/ProblemJson.cs ===
namespace RondiTrack.Tests.TestSupport;

using System.Text.Json;

// NEW 6.1: reads a problem response so two responses can be compared
public static class ProblemJson
{
    public static async Task<(int Status, string? Title, string? Detail)> ReadAsync(HttpResponseMessage response)
    {
        using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync());
        var root = doc.RootElement;
        return (
            root.GetProperty("status").GetInt32(),
            root.TryGetProperty("title", out var title) ? title.GetString() : null,
            root.TryGetProperty("detail", out var detail) ? detail.GetString() : null);
    }
}
=== RondiTrack.Tests/UntrackedReadTests.cs ===
using RondiTrack.Tests.TestSupport;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Testing;
using Xunit;

namespace RondiTrack.Tests;

// EDIT 5.2: new file. Proves a PUT still persists now that every read is AsNoTracking
// EDIT 5.3: the PUT must now send If-Match (the ETag from the POST/GET), otherwise it is a 428.
[Collection("Postgres collection")]
public class UntrackedReadTests(PostgresApiFactory factory)
{
    private readonly HttpClient _client = factory.CreateClient();

    [Fact]
    public async Task Update_User_Persists_After_Untracked_Read()
    {
        var created = await _client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/users",
            new { FullName = "Before Rename", Email = $"before{Guid.NewGuid()}@example.com" });
        var user = (await created.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<UserDto>())!;
        var etag = created.Headers.ETag!.Tag;   // EDIT 5.3: the token comes back with the response

        var newName = $"Renamed {Guid.NewGuid()}";
        var request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Put, $"/api/users/{user.Id}")
        {
            Content = JsonContent.Create(new { FullName = newName, Email = $"after{Guid.NewGuid()}@example.com" })
        };
        request.Headers.TryAddWithoutValidation("If-Match", etag);   // EDIT 5.3
        var put = await _client.SendAsync(request);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, put.StatusCode);

        var get = await _client.GetFromJsonAsync<UserDto>($"/api/users/{user.Id}");
        Assert.Equal(newName, get!.FullName);
    }

    private sealed record UserDto(Guid Id, string FullName);
}
